const FEAT_SLOTS = CATS.map(function (c) { return { stage: c.stage, cat: c.name, key: c.slug }; });

function catArticleList(catName) {
  const cat = CATS.filter(function (c) { return c.name === catName; })[0];
  if (!cat) return [];
  const sibling = CATS.filter(function (c) { return c.stage === cat.stage; }).map(function (c) { return c.name; });
  const slot = sibling.indexOf(catName);
  const cands = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.stage === cat.stage; });
  return cands.filter(function (a, i) { return i % sibling.length === slot; });
}

function topicArticleList(t) {
  const arts = catArticleList(t.category);
  return arts.filter(function (a, i) { return (t.name.length + i * 7) % 5 < 3; });
}

function slugify(t) {
  return t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
}

const TOPICS_ALL = (function () {
  const out = [];
  CATS.forEach(function (c) {
    c.topics.forEach(function (t, i) {
      const slug = slugify(t);
      const h = (t.length * 7 + i * 3) % 11;
      out.push({
        name: t, slug: slug, stage: c.stage, category: c.name,
        articles: h < 5 ? 0 : h < 8 ? 1 : h < 10 ? 2 : 3,
        active: h !== 4 && h !== 9,
        keywords: c.keywords.filter(function (k) { return k.toLowerCase() === t.toLowerCase(); })
      });
    });
  });
  return out;
})();

  componentDidMount() {
    const self = this;
    this._down = function (e) {
      const t = e.target;
      const inAccount = t && t.closest && t.closest("[data-account]");
      const inSearch = t && t.closest && t.closest("[data-search]");
      const patch = {};
      if (!inAccount && self.state.accountOpen) patch.accountOpen = false;
      if (!inSearch && self.state.query) patch.query = "";
      if (Object.keys(patch).length) self.setState(patch);
    };
    this._key = function (e) {
      if (e.key !== "Escape") return;
      const patch = {};
      if (self.state.accountOpen) patch.accountOpen = false;
      if (self.state.query) patch.query = "";
      if (self.state.navOpen) patch.navOpen = false;
      if (self.state.userDlg) patch.userDlg = null;
      if (Object.keys(patch).length) self.setState(patch);
    };
    this._resize = function () {
      const n = window.innerWidth <= 980;
      if (n !== self.state.narrow) self.setState({ narrow: n, navOpen: false });
    };
    this._resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", this._resize);
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", this._down, true);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", this._key);
    this._slideTimer = setInterval(function () {
      if (self.state.signedIn || self.state.slidePaused) return;
      self.setState(function (s) { return { slide: (s.slide + 1) % LOGIN_SLIDES.length }; });
    }, SLIDE_MS);
  }

  componentWillUnmount() {
    if (this._t) clearTimeout(this._t);
    if (this._slideTimer) clearInterval(this._slideTimer);
    if (this._down) document.removeEventListener("mousedown", this._down, true);
    if (this._key) document.removeEventListener("keydown", this._key);
    if (this._resize) window.removeEventListener("resize", this._resize);
  }

  can(key) { return PERM[key].indexOf(this.state.role) !== -1; }

  // Previewing other roles is a Supervisor tool. It is keyed to the signed-in account's own role,
  // never the role being previewed, so no other account can use it to raise its own access.
  canPreview() {
    const s = this.state;
    const mine = this.staffAccounts().filter(function (a) { return a.key === s.signedInName; })[0];
    return !!s.signedIn && !!mine && mine.role === "super_user";
  }

  // Team accounts derived from site users that hold a staff role, with any edits made in Users or Team.
  staffAccounts() {
    const s = this.state;
    const order = STAFF.map(function (m) { return m.name; });
    return USERS.filter(function (u) { return !!s.staffRole[u.name] && s.deletedUsers.indexOf(u.name) === -1; }).map(function (u) {
      const ed = s.userEdits[u.name] || {};
      return { key: u.name, name: ed.name || u.name, initials: ed.initials || u.initials, email: ed.email || u.email, role: s.staffRole[u.name] };
    }).sort(function (x, y) {
      const a = order.indexOf(x.key), b = order.indexOf(y.key);
      return (a === -1 ? 99 : a) - (b === -1 ? 99 : b);
    });
  }
  me() {
    const s = this.state, list = this.staffAccounts();
    return list.filter(function (a) { return a.key === s.signedInName; })[0] || list[0] || Object.assign({ key: ACCOUNTS[0].name }, ACCOUNTS[0]);
  }
  roleLabel(id) { return (ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r.id === id; })[0] || {}).label || "User"; }
  set(k, v) { this.setState(function (s) { const o = {}; o[k] = v; return o; }); }
  go(screen) { this.setState({ screen: screen, open: null, query: "", accountOpen: false, navOpen: false }); }

  say(msg) {
    const self = this;
    this.setState({ toast: msg });
    if (this._t) clearTimeout(this._t);
    this._t = setTimeout(function () { self.setState({ toast: "" }); }, 3200);
  }

  pill(color, bg) {
    return "display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;color:" + color + ";background:" + bg;
  }

  chip(on, color) {
    return "padding:6px 12px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " +
      (on ? color : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? color : "#fff") + ";color:" + (on ? C.ivory : C.char);
  }

  goCta(enabled) {
    return "border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:10px 18px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:" + (enabled ? "pointer" : "not-allowed") +
      ";background:" + (enabled ? C.sage700 : C.sand) + ";color:" + (enabled ? C.ivory : C.mute);
  }

  loginField() {
    return "width:100%;border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";border-radius:10px;background:#fff;padding:11px 13px;font-size:13.5px;color:" + C.char;
  }

  cta(enabled) {
    return "border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:10px 18px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:" + (enabled ? "pointer" : "not-allowed") +
      ";background:" + (enabled ? C.cta : C.sand) + ";color:" + (enabled ? C.ivory : C.mute);
  }

  box(on, color) {
    return "width:17px;height:17px;flex:none;border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:1px solid " +
      (on ? color : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? color : "#fff");
  }

  askConfirm(cfg) { this.setState({ confirm: cfg }); }

  syncFeatured(assign) {
    const titles = Object.keys(assign).map(function (k) { return assign[k] && assign[k].title; });
    ARTICLES.forEach(function (a) { a.featured = titles.indexOf(a.title) !== -1; });
    this.setState({ featAssign: assign });
  }

  addKeyword() {
    const v = (this.state.kwInput || "").trim();
    if (!v) return;
    this.setState(function (st) {
      return st.kwDraft.some(function (k) { return k.toLowerCase() === v.toLowerCase(); })
        ? { kwInput: "" } : { kwDraft: st.kwDraft.concat([v]), kwInput: "" };
    });
  }

  pgr(list, key, per) {
    const self = this, s = this.state;
    const total = list.length;
    const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / per));
    const cur = Math.min(Math.max(1, s[key] || 1), pages);
    const from = (cur - 1) * per;
    const btn = function (en) {
      return "display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;border:1px solid " + (en ? "#E8E1D7" : "#F4EEE7") +
        ";border-radius:9999px;padding:6px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;background:#fff;cursor:" + (en ? "pointer" : "not-allowed") +
        ";color:" + (en ? "#2E2E2E" : "#C4BDB3");
    };
    const set = function (n) { const o = {}; o[key] = n; self.setState(o); };
    return {
      items: list.slice(from, from + per),
      bar: {
        show: total > per,
        label: total ? "Showing " + (from + 1) + "–" + Math.min(from + per, total) + " of " + total : "Nothing to show",
        pageLabel: "Page " + cur + " of " + pages,
        prevStyle: btn(cur > 1), nextStyle: btn(cur < pages),
        prev: function () { if (cur > 1) set(cur - 1); },
        next: function () { if (cur < pages) set(cur + 1); }
      }
    };
  }

  audit(action, target) {
    const me = this.me();
    const d = new Date();
    const when = d.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" }) + ", " + d.toTimeString().slice(0, 5);
    this.setState(function (st) { return { auditLog: [{ when: when, who: me.initials, action: action, target: target }].concat(st.auditLog) }; });
  }

  setQ(fn, extra) {
    this.setState(function (st) {
      const q = fn(st.q);
      return Object.assign({ q: q, pre: q.filter(function (x) { return x.at === "pre"; }).map(function (x) { return x.id; }),
        review: q.filter(function (x) { return x.at === "review"; }).map(function (x) { return x.id; }) }, extra || {});
    });
  }

  v5editor() {
    const self = this, s = this.state;
    const me = this.me();
    const canPublish = CAN_PUBLISH.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;
    const words = function (t) { return (t || "").trim() ? t.trim().split(/\s+/).length : 0; };
    const cats = CATS.filter(function (c) { return c.stage === s.draftStage; });
    const cat = cats.filter(function (c) { return c.name === s.draftCat; })[0];
    const sel = s.draftTopicSel || [];
    const avail = cat ? cat.topics : [];
    const topicChips = avail.map(function (t) {
      const i = sel.indexOf(t), on = i !== -1;
      return { label: (i === 0 ? "★ " : "") + t + (i === 0 ? " · primary" : ""),
        style: "padding:5px 10px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (on ? (i === 0 ? C.sage900 : C.sage200) : C.sand) + ";background:" + (i === 0 ? C.sage900 : on ? C.sageSoft : "#fff") + ";color:" + (i === 0 ? C.ivory : on ? C.sage900 : C.char),
        pick: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const cur = (st.draftTopicSel || []).slice(); const k = cur.indexOf(t); if (k === -1) cur.push(t); else cur.splice(k, 1); return { draftTopicSel: cur }; }); } };
    });
    const asp = ASPECTS.filter(function (a) { return a.value === s.draftAspect; })[0];
    const SOURCES = EXTRACTED;
    const selCount = Object.keys(s.srcSel).filter(function (k) { return s.srcSel[k]; }).length;
    const srcResults = SOURCES.map(function (r, ri) {
      const open = s.srcExp === ri;
      return { src: r.src, title: r.title, date: r.date, url: r.url, open: open, toggleLabel: open ? "Hide content" : "Read extracted content",
        toggle: function () { self.setState({ srcExp: open ? null : ri }); },
        badge: r.complete ? (r.low ? "Low-weight source" : "Full text extracted") : "Incomplete extraction",
        badgeStyle: self.pill(r.complete ? (r.low ? C.clayD : C.sage700) : C.clayM, r.complete ? (r.low ? "#F7EFE9" : C.sageSoft) : "#F7EFE9"),
        warnOn: !r.complete, warnText: "Part of this page could not be read (it needs JavaScript or blocks automated access). Only the sections below were extracted.",
        sections: r.sections.map(function (x, xi) {
          const key = ri + "-" + xi, on = !!s.srcSel[key];
          return { h: x.h, t: x.t, boxStyle: self.box(on, C.moira) + ";margin-top:2px",
            style: "display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage200 : C.linen) + ";background:" + (on ? "#F2F5F3" : "#FAF7F2"),
            pick: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.srcSel); o[key] = !o[key]; return { srcSel: o }; }); } };
        }) };
    });
    const paras = (s.draftBody || "").split(/\n\s*\n/).map(function (p) { return p.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    const imgs = s.imgList;
    const mainImg = imgs.filter(function (i) { return i.role === "main"; })[0];
    const setImg = function (id, patch) { self.setState(function (st) { return { imgList: st.imgList.map(function (i) {
      if (patch.role === "main" && i.role === "main" && i.id !== id) return Object.assign({}, i, { role: "saved" });
      return i.id === id ? Object.assign({}, i, patch) : i; }) }; }); };
    const fromDraft = s.preview && s.preview.fromDraft;
    const pvSrc = fromDraft ? { title: s.draftTitle || "Untitled draft", blurb: s.draftBlurb, stage: s.draftStage || "Pregnancy", category: s.draftCat || "No category yet", preamble: s.draftPreamble } : (s.preview || {});
    const blocks = [];
    if (fromDraft) {
      (paras.length ? paras : ["No article text yet."]).forEach(function (p, i) {
        blocks.push({ isPara: true, isImg: false, text: p, marker: "Paragraph " + (i + 1) });
        imgs.filter(function (im) { return im.role === "interior" && +im.para === i + 1; }).forEach(function (im) {
          const st = im.align === "full" ? "width:100%;height:180px" : "width:46%;height:140px;float:" + im.align + ";margin:" + (im.align === "left" ? "0 16px 8px 0" : "0 0 8px 16px");
          blocks.push({ isPara: false, isImg: true, label: im.label, marker: "Interior image · after paragraph " + im.para + " · " + im.align,
            style: st + ";border-radius:10px;background-image:repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,#EDE6DE 0 7px,#F8F6F2 7px 14px);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center" });
        });
      });
    } else blocks.push({ isPara: true, isImg: false, text: "The full article text appears here.", marker: "Body" });
    const pvEdit = s.previewMode === "edit";
    const openPrev = function (mode) { return function () { self.setState({ preview: { fromDraft: true }, previewMode: mode }); }; };
    // The article being edited is tracked by the title it had when Edit was clicked, so a new draft
    // never lands on top of an existing article that happens to share its title.
    const editing = s.editingTitle ? ARTICLES.filter(function (x) { return x.title === s.editingTitle; })[0] : null;
    const titleClash = function () {
      const t = (s.draftTitle || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      return ARTICLES.filter(function (x) { return x !== editing && x.title.trim().toLowerCase() === t; })[0];
    };
    const clashMsg = function (c) { return "Another article is already called “" + c.title + "”. Give this one a different title."; };
    const persist = function (status) {
      const title = (s.draftTitle || "").trim();
      let a = editing;
      const fields = { stage: s.draftStage, aspect: asp ? asp.label : "", tier: s.draftTier, blurb: s.draftBlurb || a && a.blurb || "", category: s.draftCat, topicList: sel.slice(),
        cg: !!s.draftCg, format: s.draftFormat, preamble: s.draftPreamble, images: imgs.map(function (i) { return { label: i.label, role: i.role, para: i.para, align: i.align }; }),
        mainFocal: s.mainFocal, sidebarW: s.sidebarW, adSize: s.adSize, warn: asp ? "" : "Aspect unconfirmed", date: "13/10/2026",
        keywords: s.draftKeywords, refs: s.aiRefs };
      if (canPublish) fields.featured = !!s.draftFeat;
      if (a) {
        const was = a.title;
        Object.assign(a, fields);
        a.title = title;
        if (was !== title) {
          const fa = Object.assign({}, s.featAssign);
          Object.keys(fa).forEach(function (k) { if (fa[k] && fa[k].title === was) fa[k] = Object.assign({}, fa[k], { title: title }); });
          self.setState({ featAssign: fa });
          self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.map(function (y) { return y.title === was ? Object.assign({}, y, { title: title }) : y; }); });
        }
      } else {
        a = A(title, s.draftStage || "Pregnancy", fields.aspect, s.draftTier, status || "draft", fields);
        // New records carry their own review and web state, so nothing is filled in by position later.
        Object.assign(a, { creator: me.initials, author: me.name, medReviewed: false, reviewers: [], everLive: false, published: false, pubDate: "", _v5: true });
        ARTICLES.unshift(a);
      }
      if (status) a.status = status;
      return a;
    };
    const clearDraft = { editingTitle: null, draftTitle: "", draftStage: "", draftAspect: "", draftTier: "", draftBody: "", draftInitials: "", draftBlurb: "", draftPreamble: "", draftCat: "", draftTopicSel: [], draftCg: false, draftFormat: "Article", imgList: [], srcSel: {}, srcRan: false, draftKeywords: "", draftFeat: false, aiRefs: "", refFiles: [] };
    const initialsOk = (s.draftInitials || "").trim().toUpperCase() === (me.initials || "").toUpperCase();
    const ready = !!s.draftTitle && !!s.draftStage && !!s.draftAspect && !!s.draftTier && initialsOk;
    // Same rule as Publish in the article list: clinical content needs a medical review before it goes live.
    const needsMed = ((!!asp && asp.label === "Medical & Clinical") || s.draftTier === "clinical_adjacent" || s.draftTier === "scientific") && !(editing && editing.medReviewed);
    const directPublish = canPublish && !needsMed;
    return {
      creatorText: "Created by " + me.name + " (" + me.initials + ") · recorded automatically",
      creatorInitials: me.initials,
      draftBlurb: s.draftBlurb, onBlurb: function (e) { self.set("draftBlurb", e.target.value); },
      blurbCount: words(s.draftBlurb) + " / 40 words", blurbColor: words(s.draftBlurb) > 45 ? C.clayM : C.mute,
      draftPreamble: s.draftPreamble, onPreamble: function (e) { self.set("draftPreamble", e.target.value); },
      draftVoice: s.draftVoice, onVoice: function (e) { self.set("draftVoice", e.target.value); },
      voiceOptions: ["BestBaby voice", "Moira — warm and personal", "Plain and clinical"],
      aiPrompts: AI_PROMPTS.map(function (t, i) {
        const on = s.aiPicks.indexOf(i) !== -1;
        return { text: (on ? "✓ " : "") + t, pick: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const p = st.aiPicks.slice(); const k = p.indexOf(i); if (k === -1) p.push(i); else p.splice(k, 1); return { aiPicks: p }; }); },
          style: "text-align:left;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.45;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.moira : C.sage200) + ";background:" + (on ? "#fff" : "rgba(255,255,255,.55)") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage900 : "#3A5540") + ";font-weight:" + (on ? "600" : "400") };
      }),
      aiRefs: s.aiRefs, onAiRefs: function (e) { self.set("aiRefs", e.target.value); },
      onRefFile: function (e) { const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if (f) self.setState(function (st) { return { refFiles: st.refFiles.concat([f.name]) }; }); },
      refFilesText: s.refFiles.length ? "Files: " + s.refFiles.join(", ") : "Optional — .txt .md .html .csv .json .pdf .docx",
      aiPickText: s.aiPicks.length + (s.aiPicks.length === 1 ? " instruction" : " instructions") + (s.aiCustom.trim() ? " + custom" : "") + " selected",
      rewrite: function () {
        if (!s.draftBody) { self.say("Write or generate some text first."); return; }
        if (!s.aiPicks.length && !s.aiCustom.trim()) { self.say("Pick at least one instruction or write your own."); return; }
        const refs = s.aiRefs.split(/\n/).filter(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).length + s.refFiles.length;
        self.say("Rewriting in " + s.draftVoice + " with " + (s.aiPicks.length + (s.aiCustom.trim() ? 1 : 0)) + " instruction(s)" + (refs ? " and " + refs + " reference(s)" : "") + ". The result replaces nothing until you accept it.");
      },
      // taxonomy
      formatOptions: ["Article", "Guide", "Checklist", "Explainer", "Video"], draftFormat: s.draftFormat, onFormat: function (e) { self.set("draftFormat", e.target.value); },
      catOptions: cats.map(function (c) { return c.name; }), draftCat: s.draftCat, catDisabled: !s.draftStage,
      catPlaceholder: s.draftStage ? "— Select a category —" : "Pick a stage first",
      onCat: function (e) { self.setState({ draftCat: e.target.value, draftTopicSel: [] }); },
      topicChips: topicChips, topicHint: !cat ? "Pick a stage and category to load topics" : sel.length ? "The first one you pick is the primary topic" : "Pick one or more — the first is primary",
      toggleCg: function () { self.set("draftCg", !s.draftCg); }, cgBox: self.box(!!s.draftCg, C.indigo), cgText: s.draftCg ? "Flagged" : "Not flagged",
      featNote: canPublish ? "Featured articles fill the stage's featured slots once live." : "Only a Supervisor or Tech can feature an article.",
      toggleFeat: function () { if (!canPublish) { self.say("Only a Supervisor or Tech can feature an article."); return; } self.set("draftFeat", !s.draftFeat); },
      featBox: self.box(!!s.draftFeat, C.clay), featText: s.draftFeat ? "Featured" : "Not featured",
      featDisabled: !canPublish, featTitle: canPublish ? "" : "Only a Supervisor or Tech can feature an article.",
      featCursor: canPublish ? "pointer" : "not-allowed", featOpacity: canPublish ? "1" : ".5",
      stageChips: Object.keys(STAGE_COLOR).map(function (k) {
        const on = s.draftStage === k;
        return { label: k, style: self.chip(on, STAGE_COLOR[k]), pick: function () { self.setState({ draftStage: on ? "" : k, draftCat: "", draftTopicSel: [] }); } };
      }),
      // sources
      srcOpen: s.srcOpenPanel, srcToggleLabel: s.srcOpenPanel ? "Hide" : "Show", toggleSrc: function () { self.set("srcOpenPanel", !s.srcOpenPanel); },
      srcCriteria: [["Stage", s.draftStage || "any"], ["Category", s.draftCat || "any"], ["C&G", s.draftCg ? "yes" : "no"], ["Topics", sel.length ? sel.join(", ") : "any"], ["Aspect", asp ? asp.label : "any"], ["Keywords", s.draftKeywords.trim() || "none"]].map(function (x) { return { k: x[0], v: x[1] }; }),
      srcQ: s.srcQ, onSrcQ: function (e) { self.set("srcQ", e.target.value); },
      draftKeywords: s.draftKeywords, onKeywords: function (e) { self.set("draftKeywords", e.target.value); },
      runSrc: function () { self.setState({ srcRan: true, srcExp: 0, srcQ: s.srcQ || s.draftKeywords.split(",")[0].trim() }); self.say("Found " + SOURCES.length + " sources across parenting sites."); },
      srcRan: s.srcRan, srcResults: srcResults,
      srcSelText: selCount ? selCount + " section" + (selCount > 1 ? "s" : "") + " selected" : "Select the sections Morpheus should draw on",
      genSrcStyle: self.goCta(selCount > 0),
      genFromSrc: function () {
        if (!selCount) { self.say("Select at least one section first."); return; }
        const picked = [];
        SOURCES.forEach(function (r, ri) { r.sections.forEach(function (x, xi) { if (s.srcSel[ri + "-" + xi]) picked.push(x); }); });
        const title = s.draftTitle || (s.srcQ ? s.srcQ.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.srcQ.slice(1) : picked[0].h);
        const bw = picked.map(function (x) { return x.t; }).join(" ").split(/\s+/).slice(0, 40).join(" ");
        self.setState({ draftTitle: title, draftBlurb: bw.replace(/[,.;:]?$/, "."), draftPreamble: picked[0].t,
          draftBody: picked.map(function (x) { return x.h + ". " + x.t; }).join("\n\n") });
        self.say("Drafted a title, slug, blurb and " + picked.length + " paragraph" + (picked.length > 1 ? "s" : "") + " from your selection. Edit before submitting.");
      },
      // images
      imgQ: s.imgQ, onImgQ: function (e) { self.set("imgQ", e.target.value); },
      searchImgs: function () {
        const kw = (s.imgQ || s.draftTitle || "parenting").trim();
        self.setState(function (st) { return { imgList: st.imgList.filter(function (i) { return i.role; }).concat([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(function (n) { return { id: "s" + Date.now() + n, label: kw.slice(0, 18) + " · stock " + n, kind: "Stock", role: "", para: "1", align: "right" }; })) }; });
      },
      imgAiOpen: s.imgAiOpen, imgAiLabel: s.imgAiOpen ? "Hide AI image" : "Generate with AI", toggleImgAi: function () {
        const rec = "Warm, natural-light editorial photo for “" + (s.draftTitle || "a BestBaby article") + "”" + (s.draftKeywords.trim() ? " — " + s.draftKeywords.trim() : "") + ". Real parents, no text, calm palette.";
        self.setState({ imgAiOpen: !s.imgAiOpen, imgPrompt: s.imgPrompt || rec });
      },
      imgPrompt: s.imgPrompt, onImgPrompt: function (e) { self.set("imgPrompt", e.target.value); },
      onImgRef: function (e) { const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if (f) self.set("imgRef", f.name); },
      imgRefText: s.imgRef ? "Reference: " + s.imgRef : "Optional — where the image service supports it",
      genImg: function () {
        if (!s.imgPrompt.trim()) { self.say("Write a prompt first."); return; }
        self.setState(function (st) { return { imgList: st.imgList.concat([1, 2].map(function (n) { return { id: "a" + Date.now() + n, label: "AI · variation " + n, kind: "AI", role: "", para: "1", align: "right" }; })) }; });
        self.say("Two AI images generated" + (s.imgRef ? " using your reference." : "."));
      },
      hasImgs: imgs.length > 0,
      imgs: imgs.map(function (i) {
        return Object.assign({}, i, { isInterior: i.role === "interior", border: i.role === "main" ? C.sage700 : i.role ? C.sage200 : C.sand, kindBg: i.kind === "AI" ? C.indigo : C.sage700,
          onRole: function (e) { setImg(i.id, { role: e.target.value }); }, onPara: function (e) { setImg(i.id, { para: e.target.value }); }, onAlign: function (e) { setImg(i.id, { align: e.target.value }); } });
      }),
      paraOptions: (paras.length ? paras : [""]).map(function (p, i) { return { value: String(i + 1), label: "After paragraph " + (i + 1) }; }),
      mainFocal: s.mainFocal, onFocal: function (e) { self.set("mainFocal", e.target.value); },
      sidebarW: s.sidebarW, onSidebarW: function (e) { self.set("sidebarW", e.target.value); },
      adSize: s.adSize, onAdSize: function (e) { self.set("adSize", e.target.value); },
      imgSummary: (mainImg ? "Main: " + mainImg.label : "No main image yet") + " · " + imgs.filter(function (i) { return i.role === "interior"; }).length + " interior · " + imgs.filter(function (i) { return i.role === "saved"; }).length + " saved",
      // preview
      openEditPreview: openPrev("edit"), openPublicPreview: openPrev("public"),
      previewOpen: !!s.preview, closePreview: function () { self.set("preview", null); },
      previewModes: [["edit", "Editing preview"], ["public", "Public preview"]].map(function (m) {
        const on = s.previewMode === m[0];
        return { label: m[1], go: function () { self.set("previewMode", m[0]); }, style: "padding:7px 14px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage900 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sage900 : "#fff") + ";color:" + (on ? C.ivory : C.char) };
      }),
      previewNote: pvEdit ? "Markers show where images sit and what each block is. Readers never see them." : "Exactly what readers will see. No editing controls.",
      pvEdit: pvEdit,
      pv: { title: pvSrc.title || "", blurb: pvSrc.blurb || "", preamble: pvSrc.preamble || "", stage: pvSrc.stage || "", category: pvSrc.category || "", stageColor: STAGE_COLOR[pvSrc.stage] || C.sage900,
        mainLabel: fromDraft ? (mainImg ? mainImg.label : "No main image chosen") : "Main image", focal: s.mainFocal, sidebar: s.sidebarW, ad: s.adSize },
      pvInterior: imgs.filter(function (i) { return i.role === "interior"; }).map(function (i) {
        return { label: i.label, para: i.para, align: i.align, onPara: function (e) { setImg(i.id, { para: e.target.value }); }, onAlign: function (e) { setImg(i.id, { align: e.target.value }); } };
      }),
      pvNoInterior: !imgs.some(function (i) { return i.role === "interior"; }),
      pvSizeHint: words(s.draftBody) + " words — " + (words(s.draftBody) > 900 ? "a long article can carry a 380px Moira sidebar and a 300 × 600 ad" : words(s.draftBody) > 450 ? "340px sidebar and a 300 × 250 ad suit this length" : "keep the sidebar at 300px; consider no ad"),
      pvBlocks: blocks, pvHasAd: s.adSize !== "none", pvAdH: s.adSize === "300 × 600" ? "260px" : "140px",
      // save / submit with persistence
      saveDraft: function () {
        if (!s.draftTitle) { self.say("Nothing to save yet — add a title first."); return; }
        const clash = titleClash();
        if (clash) { self.say(clashMsg(clash)); return; }
        const a = persist(); self.setState({ editingTitle: a.title });
        self.say("Draft saved with its taxonomy, C&G flag, creator and image placement.");
      },
      readyText: ready
        ? (directPublish ? "Ready to publish" : canPublish ? "Ready — needs a medical review before it can go live" : "Ready to submit for review")
        : "Still needed: " + [!s.draftTitle && "title", !s.draftStage && "stage", !s.draftAspect && "aspect", !s.draftTier && "evidence tier",
          !s.draftInitials ? "initials" : !initialsOk && "your own initials (" + me.initials + ")"].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
      submitLabel: directPublish ? "Publish" : canPublish ? "Submit for medical review" : "Submit for review",
      submitStyle: self.goCta(ready),
      readyColor: ready ? C.sage700 : C.clayM,
      initialsHint: "Must match your account initials (" + me.initials + ").",
      submitDraft: function () {
        if (!ready) { self.say(s.draftInitials && !initialsOk ? "Sign off with your own initials (" + me.initials + ")." : "Complete the required fields first."); return; }
        const clash = titleClash();
        if (clash) { self.say(clashMsg(clash)); return; }
        // A live article sent back for review keeps its web status; only new or draft work is held as a draft.
        const a = persist(directPublish ? "live" : (editing && editing.status === "live" ? undefined : "draft"));
        if (directPublish) { a.everLive = true; a.published = true; a.pubDate = "13/10/2026"; self.audit("Published", a.title); self.setState(Object.assign({ screen: "articles", artStatus: "live", pgArt: 1, open: null }, clearDraft)); self.say("Published. It is live on the site now."); return; }
        self.setQ(function (qq) { return [{ id: Date.now(), kind: "Article", title: a.title, stage: a.stage, aspect: a.aspect, tier: (TIERS.filter(function (t) { return t.id === a.tier; })[0] || TIERS[4]).label,
          by: me.name, when: "just now", words: String(words(s.draftBody)), blurb: a.blurb, at: "review", notes: [] }].concat(qq.filter(function (x) { return x.title !== a.title; })); },
          Object.assign({ screen: "articles", pgArt: 1, open: null }, clearDraft));
        self.say("Submitted. A reviewer will see it in For review.");
      }
    };
  }

  v5more() {
    const self = this, s = this.state;
    const me = this.me();
    const allowed = this.can(s.screen);
    const canApprove = CAN_APPROVE.indexOf(s.role) !== -1, canPublish = CAN_PUBLISH.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;
    // studio
    const stageTopics = CATS.filter(function (c) { return !s.mStage || c.stage === s.mStage; }).reduce(function (a, c) { return a.concat(c.topics); }, []);
    const diag = s.diagRan
      ? [{ name: "OpenAI web search", state: "Working", dot: C.sage700 }, { name: "Google Custom Search", state: "Failing", dot: C.clayM }]
      : [{ name: "OpenAI web search", state: "Not checked", dot: C.mute }, { name: "Google Custom Search", state: "Not checked", dot: C.mute }];
    const out = {
      diagRows: diag, diagOpen: s.diagOpen,
      runDiag: function () { self.setState({ diagRan: true, diagOpen: true }); },
      diagReport: [
        { k: "OpenAI web search", v: "200 OK · 1.4s · 10 results" },
        { k: "Google Custom Search", v: "403 Forbidden · the API key is not enabled for the Custom Search JSON API" },
        { k: "Search engine ID", v: "Set" },
        { k: "Suggested fix", v: "Enable the Custom Search API for this key in Google Cloud, then run diagnostics again" }
      ],
      mStage: s.mStage, mStageOpts: Object.keys(STAGE_COLOR), onMStage: function (e) { self.setState({ mStage: e.target.value, mTopic: "" }); },
      mTopic: s.mTopic, mTopicOpts: stageTopics, onMTopic: function (e) { self.set("mTopic", e.target.value); },
      mKw: s.mKw, onMKw: function (e) { self.set("mKw", e.target.value); },
      genQueries: function () {
        const base = (s.mKw || s.mTopic || s.mStage || "pregnancy sleep").trim();
        const st = s.mStage ? s.mStage.toLowerCase() : "pregnancy";
        self.setState({ mQueries: [base, base + " NHS advice", "is " + base + " safe", base + " what to expect", base + " tips from midwives", base + " " + st + " research"], mQSel: [] });
      },
      hasQueries: s.mQueries.length > 0,
      mQueries: s.mQueries.map(function (t) {
        const on = s.trends.indexOf(t) !== -1;
        return { text: t, boxStyle: self.box(on, C.moira),
          style: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sageSoft : "#fff") + ";color:" + C.char,
          pick: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const tr = st.trends.slice(); const k = tr.indexOf(t); if (k === -1) tr.push(t); else tr.splice(k, 1); return { trends: tr }; }); } };
      }),
      // Step 2 reads each source in full, like the New article source search, so sections can be highlighted.
      sources: EXTRACTED.map(function (r, i) {
        const on = s.sources.indexOf(i) !== -1, reading = s.mRead === i;
        return { title: r.title, meta: r.src + " · " + r.date, url: r.url, on: on, boxStyle: self.box(on, C.moira),
          style: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sageSoft : "#fff"),
          toggle: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const a = st.sources.slice(); const k = a.indexOf(i); if (k === -1) a.push(i); else a.splice(k, 1); return { sources: a }; }); },
          reading: reading, readLabel: reading ? "Hide full article" : "Read full article",
          read: function () { self.set("mRead", reading ? null : i); },
          badge: r.complete ? (r.low ? "Low-weight source" : "Full text extracted") : "Incomplete extraction",
          badgeStyle: self.pill(r.complete ? (r.low ? C.clayD : C.sage700) : C.clayM, r.complete ? (r.low ? "#F7EFE9" : C.sageSoft) : "#F7EFE9"),
          warnOn: !r.complete, warnText: "Part of this page could not be read (it needs JavaScript or blocks automated access). Only the sections below were extracted.",
          sections: r.sections.map(function (x, xi) {
            const key = i + "-" + xi, sel = !!s.mSecSel[key];
            return { h: x.h, t: x.t, boxStyle: self.box(sel, C.moira) + ";margin-top:2px",
              style: "display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (sel ? C.sage200 : C.linen) + ";background:" + (sel ? "#F2F5F3" : "#FAF7F2"),
              pick: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.mSecSel); o[key] = !o[key]; return { mSecSel: o }; }); } };
          }) };
      }),
      voiceDials: Object.keys(s.dials).map(function (k) {
        const n = s.dials[k];
        const val = k === "Reading age" ? (n < 34 ? "9–11" : n < 67 ? "12–14" : "15+") : (n < 34 ? "Low" : n < 67 ? "Medium" : "High");
        return { label: k, n: String(n), value: val, onChange: function (e) { const v = +e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { const d = Object.assign({}, st.dials); d[k] = v; return { dials: d }; }); } };
      }),
      generate: function () {
        const title = s.genTitle || s.trends[0] || "Untitled draft";
        const picked = [];
        EXTRACTED.forEach(function (r, ri) { r.sections.forEach(function (x, xi) { if (s.mSecSel[ri + "-" + xi]) picked.push(x); }); });
        const from = picked.length ? picked : EXTRACTED.filter(function (r, i) { return s.sources.indexOf(i) !== -1; }).map(function (r) { return r.sections[0]; });
        const blurb = from.map(function (x) { return x.t; }).join(" ").split(/\s+/).slice(0, 40).join(" ").replace(/[,.;:]?$/, ".");
        self.setState({ screen: "new", open: null, query: "", editingTitle: null, draftTitle: title, draftStage: s.genStage, draftAspect: s.genAspect, draftTier: "ai_created", draftInitials: "",
          draftBlurb: from.length ? blurb : "", draftPreamble: from.length ? from[0].t : "", draftCat: "", draftTopicSel: [], draftKeywords: s.trends.join(", "),
          draftBody: (from.length ? from.map(function (x) { return x.h + ". " + x.t; }).join("\n\n") + "\n\n" : "") +
            "Morpheus drafted this from " + s.sources.length + " source" + (s.sources.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + (picked.length ? " and " + picked.length + " highlighted section" + (picked.length === 1 ? "" : "s") : "") + ", tuned to warmth " + s.dials.Warmth + "/100. Edit it in your own voice before submitting — nothing is published until a human signs it off." });
        self.say("Draft generated with a title, slug, blurb and preamble, and opened in the same editor as New article.");
      }
    };
    // users — classification is the team role, so Users and Team always agree
    const CLASSES = ["User"].concat(ROLES.map(function (r) { return r.label; }));
    const classOf = function (u) { return s.staffRole[u.name] ? self.roleLabel(s.staffRole[u.name]) : "User"; };
    const dlgU = USERS.filter(function (x) { return x.name === s.userDlg; })[0];
    const dd = s.dlgDraft || {};
    const edOf = function (u) { return s.userEdits[u.name] || {}; };
    const delUser = function (u) {
      if (u.name === me.key) { self.say("You cannot delete your own account."); return; }
      self.askConfirm({ title: "Delete this user?", body: (edOf(u).name || u.name) + " (" + (edOf(u).email || u.email) + ") and their saved data will be erased" + (s.staffRole[u.name] ? ", and they are removed from the team" : "") + ". A record of the deletion (data category and date) is kept for legal purposes; the content itself is not. This cannot be undone.", label: "Delete user",
        run: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const sr = Object.assign({}, st.staffRole); delete sr[u.name]; return { deletedUsers: st.deletedUsers.concat([u.name]), staffRole: sr, userDlg: null, dlgDraft: {} }; }); self.audit("Deleted user", u.name); self.say(u.name + " deleted."); } });
    };
    Object.assign(out, {
      userClass: s.userClass, userClasses: ["All classifications"].concat(CLASSES),
      onUserClass: function (e) { self.setState({ userClass: e.target.value, pgUsers: 1 }); },
      classOptions: CLASSES,
      dlgName: dd.name != null ? dd.name : dlgU ? (edOf(dlgU).name || dlgU.name) : "",
      dlgPlace: dd.place != null ? dd.place : dlgU ? (edOf(dlgU).place || dlgU.place) : "",
      dlgInitials: dd.initials != null ? dd.initials : dlgU ? (edOf(dlgU).initials || dlgU.initials) : "",
      dlgEmail: dd.email != null ? dd.email : dlgU ? (edOf(dlgU).email || dlgU.email) : "",
      dlgClass: dd.cls || (dlgU ? classOf(dlgU) : ""),
      dlgNote: dlgU && s.staffRole[dlgU.name] ? "Team account: initials, email and role here are the same ones shown in Team. Passwords are managed in Team." : "Site user: give them a role to add them to the team.",
      onDlgName: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { return { dlgDraft: Object.assign({}, st.dlgDraft, { name: v }) }; }); },
      onDlgPlace: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { return { dlgDraft: Object.assign({}, st.dlgDraft, { place: v }) }; }); },
      onDlgInitials: function (e) { const v = e.target.value.toUpperCase().slice(0, 3); self.setState(function (st) { return { dlgDraft: Object.assign({}, st.dlgDraft, { initials: v }) }; }); },
      onDlgEmail: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { return { dlgDraft: Object.assign({}, st.dlgDraft, { email: v }) }; }); },
      onDlgClass: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { return { dlgDraft: Object.assign({}, st.dlgDraft, { cls: v }) }; }); },
      dlgSave: function () {
        if (!dlgU) return;
        const ed = Object.assign({}, s.userEdits), sr = Object.assign({}, s.staffRole), pw = Object.assign({}, s.staffPw);
        const prev = ed[dlgU.name] || {};
        ed[dlgU.name] = {
          name: dd.name != null ? dd.name : prev.name, place: dd.place != null ? dd.place : prev.place,
          initials: dd.initials != null ? (dd.initials || prev.initials) : prev.initials, email: dd.email != null ? (dd.email.trim() || prev.email) : prev.email
        };
        const was = classOf(dlgU);
        if (dd.cls && dd.cls !== was) {
          if (dlgU.name === me.key && sr[dlgU.name] === "super_user") { self.say("You cannot change your own Supervisor role."); return; }
          const r = ROLES.filter(function (x) { return x.label === dd.cls; })[0];
          if (r) { sr[dlgU.name] = r.id; if (!pw[dlgU.name]) pw[dlgU.name] = DEMO_PASSWORD; } else delete sr[dlgU.name];
          self.audit("Changed classification", dlgU.name + ": " + was + " → " + dd.cls);
        }
        self.setState({ userEdits: ed, staffRole: sr, staffPw: pw, dlgDraft: {} });
        self.say("Profile saved." + (dd.cls && dd.cls !== was ? " Team updated to match." : ""));
      },
      dlgReset: function () { if (dlgU) { self.audit("Sent password reset", dlgU.name); self.say("Password reset link sent to " + (edOf(dlgU).email || dlgU.email) + "."); } },
      dlgDelete: function () { if (dlgU) delUser(dlgU); },
      delUser: delUser,
      rolePerms: ROLES.map(function (r) { return { label: r.label, color: r.bg === C.linen || r.bg === C.sand ? C.clay : r.bg, points: r.points }; }),
      exportUsers: function () {
        const rows = [["Name", "Email", "Initials", "Classification", "Stage", "Status", "Location", "Joined", "Account"]].concat(USERS.filter(function (u) { return s.deletedUsers.indexOf(u.name) === -1; }).map(function (u) {
          const st = s.suspended.indexOf(u.name) !== -1 ? "Suspended" : s.pendingBeta.indexOf(u.name) !== -1 ? "Pending" : "Active";
          return [edOf(u).name || u.name, edOf(u).email || u.email, edOf(u).initials || u.initials, classOf(u), u.stage, st, edOf(u).place || u.place, u.joined, u.account];
        }));
        const csv = rows.map(function (r) { return r.map(function (c) { return '"' + String(c).replace(/"/g, '""') + '"'; }).join(","); }).join("\n");
        try {
          const a = document.createElement("a");
          a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv" }));
          a.download = "bestbaby-users.csv";
          document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
        } catch (err) { /* download blocked: the toast still confirms the export */ }
        self.audit("Exported users", (rows.length - 1) + " accounts");
        self.say((rows.length - 1) + " accounts exported as CSV.");
      }
    });
    // team — PIN-protected; members are the site users that hold a staff role
    const team = self.staffAccounts();
    const privateDocs = DOCS.filter(function (d) { return s.docDel.indexOf(d[0]) === -1 && (s.docLibMap[d[0]] || d[2]) === "Private papers"; });
    Object.assign(out, {
      teamLocked: !s.teamUnlocked, teamOpen: !!s.teamUnlocked,
      teamPin: s.teamPin, teamPinErr: s.teamPinErr,
      onTeamPin: function (e) { self.setState({ teamPin: e.target.value, teamPinErr: "" }); },
      teamUnlock: function (e) {
        if (e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
        if (s.teamPin === TEAM_PIN) { self.setState({ teamUnlocked: true, teamPin: "", teamPinErr: "" }); self.audit("Opened Team", "PIN accepted"); return; }
        self.setState({ teamPinErr: "That is not the Team password.", teamPin: "" });
      },
      roleOptions: ROLES.map(function (r) { return { id: r.id, label: r.label }; }),
      teamRows: team.map(function (m) {
        const r = ROLES.filter(function (x) { return x.id === m.role; })[0] || ROLES[0];
        const current = s.role === m.role;
        const pw = s.staffPw[m.key] || "";
        const shown = !!s.pwShow[m.key], editing = s.pwEdit === m.key;
        return { name: m.name, initials: m.initials, role: m.role, reach: r.powers, email: m.email, bg: current ? C.ivory : "#fff",
          roleOpts: ROLES.map(function (x) { return { id: x.id, label: x.label, sel: x.id === m.role }; }),
          onInitials: function (ev) { const v = ev.target.value.toUpperCase().slice(0, 3); self.setState(function (st) { const t = Object.assign({}, st.userEdits); t[m.key] = Object.assign({}, t[m.key], { initials: v }); return { userEdits: t }; }); },
          onRole: function (ev) {
            const v = ev.target.value;
            if (m.key === me.key && m.role === "super_user" && v !== "super_user") { self.say("You cannot remove your own Supervisor role."); return; }
            self.setState(function (st) { const t = Object.assign({}, st.staffRole); t[m.key] = v; return { staffRole: t }; });
            self.audit("Changed role", m.name + " → " + self.roleLabel(v));
          },
          pwShown: shown ? pw : pw.replace(/./g, "•"), pwToggleLabel: shown ? "Hide" : "Show",
          togglePw: function () { self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.pwShow); o[m.key] = !o[m.key]; return { pwShow: o }; }); },
          pwEditing: editing, pwNotEditing: !editing, pwDraft: editing ? s.pwDraft : "",
          editPw: function () { self.setState({ pwEdit: m.key, pwDraft: "" }); },
          onPwDraft: function (ev) { self.set("pwDraft", ev.target.value); },
          savePw: function () {
            const v = (s.pwDraft || "").trim();
            if (v.length < 6) { self.say("Use at least 6 characters."); return; }
            self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.staffPw); o[m.key] = v; return { staffPw: o, pwEdit: null, pwDraft: "" }; });
            self.audit("Changed password", m.name); self.say("Password changed for " + m.name + ".");
          },
          openUser: function () { self.setState({ screen: "users", userDlg: m.key, dlgDraft: {} }); },
          previewLabel: current ? "Current" : "Preview",
          previewStyle: "border-radius:9999px;padding:5px 11px;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;cursor:" + (current ? "default" : "pointer") + ";border:1px solid " + (current ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (current ? C.sageSoft : "#fff") + ";color:" + (current ? C.sage700 : C.char),
          preview: function () {
            if (current) return;
            if (!self.canPreview()) { self.say("Only a Supervisor can preview other roles."); return; }
            self.setState({ role: m.role, screen: PERM.articles.indexOf(m.role) !== -1 ? "articles" : "review", backfillOpen: false }); self.say("Previewing the panel as " + r.label + ".");
          } };
      }),
      teamCandidates: USERS.filter(function (u) { return !s.staffRole[u.name] && s.deletedUsers.indexOf(u.name) === -1; }).map(function (u) { return u.name; }),
      teamAddPick: s.teamAddPick, onTeamAddPick: function (e) { self.set("teamAddPick", e.target.value); },
      teamAdd: function () {
        if (!s.teamAddPick) { self.say("Pick a site user first."); return; }
        const n = s.teamAddPick;
        self.setState(function (st) { const t = Object.assign({}, st.staffRole), pw = Object.assign({}, st.staffPw); t[n] = "content_editor"; if (!pw[n]) pw[n] = DEMO_PASSWORD; return { staffRole: t, staffPw: pw, teamAddPick: "" }; });
        self.audit("Added to team", n); self.say(n + " added to the team as a Content Editor. Change the role or password here.");
      },
      privateDocs: privateDocs.map(function (d) {
        const full = d[0] + "." + d[1];
        return { name: full, size: d[3], date: d[4] + " 2026",
          view: function () { self.say("Opening " + full + " in the viewer."); },
          del: function () { self.askConfirm({ title: "Delete this file?", body: "“" + full + "” will be removed from Private papers. This cannot be undone.", label: "Delete file",
            run: function () { self.setState(function (st) { return { docDel: st.docDel.concat([d[0]]) }; }); self.audit("Deleted private paper", full); self.say(full + " deleted."); } }); } };
      }),
      noPrivateDocs: privateDocs.length === 0,
      uploadPrivate: function () { self.setState({ upOpen: true, upFile: null, upErr: "", upLib: "Private papers", upType: "Reference" }); }
    });
    // notifications — beta applications (Page 1D) are emailed to App@BestBaby.org
    const RULES = [
      ["beta", "Beta application received", "App@BestBaby.org"], ["story", "Story submitted from the public site", "Content Editors"],
      ["safety", "Safety flag (Tier 3 or 4)", "Supervisor and Tech · always on", true], ["review", "Article submitted for review", "Reviewers"],
      ["changes", "Article returned with changes", "The author"], ["pre", "Approved into Pre-Published", "Supervisor"], ["pub", "Article published", "The author and reviewer"]
    ];
    const appState = function (b) {
      if (s.betaDecided[b.name]) return s.betaDecided[b.name];
      const u = USERS.filter(function (x) { return x.name === b.name; })[0];
      return u && s.pendingBeta.indexOf(b.name) === -1 ? "Approved" : "Pending";
    };
    Object.assign(out, {
      isNotifications: allowed && s.screen === "notifications", isAudit: allowed && s.screen === "audit",
      notifRules: RULES.map(function (r) {
        const on = r[3] || !s.notifOff[r[0]];
        return { name: r[1], to: r[2], locked: !!r[3], pressed: on ? "true" : "false", title: r[3] ? "Safety alerts cannot be turned off" : "",
          style: "flex:none;width:44px;height:26px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage700 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sage700 : C.linen) + ";display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:" + (on ? "flex-end" : "flex-start") + ";padding:2px;cursor:" + (r[3] ? "not-allowed" : "pointer") + ";opacity:" + (r[3] ? ".7" : "1"),
          knob: "width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:999px;background:#fff;display:block",
          toggle: function () { if (r[3]) return; self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.notifOff); o[r[0]] = !o[r[0]]; return { notifOff: o }; }); } };
      }),
      betaApps: BETA_APPS.map(function (b) {
        const st = appState(b), pending = st === "Pending";
        return { name: b.first + " " + b.last, status: st, pending: pending,
          statusStyle: self.pill(pending ? C.charL : st === "Approved" ? C.sage700 : C.clayM, pending ? C.linen : st === "Approved" ? C.sageSoft : "#F7EFE9"),
          meta: "Applied " + b.when + " · emailed to App@BestBaby.org",
          why: b.why,
          fields: [["Email", b.email], ["Country", b.country], ["What should we call you", b.call], ["Healthcare or childcare professional", b.hcp], ["Heard about BestBaby", b.heard], ["Cookies", b.cookies]].map(function (f) { return { k: f[0], v: f[1] }; }),
          approve: function () {
            self.setState(function (st2) { const d = Object.assign({}, st2.betaDecided); d[b.name] = "Approved"; return { betaDecided: d, pendingBeta: st2.pendingBeta.filter(function (n) { return n !== b.name; }) }; });
            self.audit("Approved beta application", b.name); self.say(b.name + " approved. An invitation to Basic Registration has been sent to " + b.email + ".");
          },
          decline: function () {
            self.askConfirm({ title: "Decline this application?", body: b.name + " will be told the beta is full for now. Only a minimal record that they applied is kept, so they are not contacted again by mistake.", label: "Decline",
              run: function () { self.setState(function (st2) { const d = Object.assign({}, st2.betaDecided); d[b.name] = "Declined"; return { betaDecided: d }; }); self.audit("Declined beta application", b.name); self.say("Application from " + b.name + " declined."); } });
          } };
      }),
      betaAppsNote: BETA_APPS.filter(function (b) { return appState(b) === "Pending"; }).length + " waiting · fields as submitted on Join the Beta",
      notifRecent: BETA_APPS.map(function (b) { return { text: b.first + " " + b.last + " applied to the beta. Email sent to App@BestBaby.org.", when: b.when }; })
        .concat([{ text: "Story submitted: “Our first fortnight in special care”.", when: "05/04/2026" }, { text: "Tier 4 safety flag raised and escalated.", when: "11 minutes ago" }]),
      auditQ: s.auditQ, onAuditQ: function (e) { self.set("auditQ", e.target.value); }
    });
    const aq = (s.auditQ || "").toLowerCase();
    const arows = s.auditLog.filter(function (a) { return !aq || (a.who + " " + a.action + " " + a.target).toLowerCase().indexOf(aq) !== -1; });
    out.auditRows = arows; out.auditEmpty = arows.length === 0;
    // stories — reviewed, then pre-published, or published straight to the Blog or Forums by the Supervisor
    Object.assign(out, {
      storyAckText: s.storyAck ? "GDPR acknowledgement given by the submitter" : "No GDPR acknowledgement — this story cannot be accepted",
      storyAckColor: s.storyAck ? C.sage700 : C.clayM,
      storyAckToggle: s.storyAck ? "Preview without acknowledgement" : "Preview with acknowledgement",
      toggleStoryAck: function () { self.set("storyAck", !s.storyAck); },
      storyDest: s.storyDest, onStoryDest: function (e) { self.set("storyDest", e.target.value); },
      storyRoutes: [
        { label: "For review", ok: self.can("new"), screen: "review", at: "review" },
        { label: "Pre-Published", ok: canApprove && s.role !== "content_editor", screen: "pre", at: "pre" },
        { label: "Publish now", ok: s.role === "super_user", screen: "stories", at: "pub" }
      ].filter(function (r) { return r.ok; }).map(function (r) {
        const dis = !s.storyAck;
        return { label: r.label, title: dis ? "Needs the submitter's GDPR acknowledgement first" : "",
          style: "border-radius:9999px;padding:6px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:" + (dis ? "not-allowed" : "pointer") + ";border:1px solid " + (dis ? C.sand : C.sage700) + ";background:" + (dis ? "#fff" : C.sage700) + ";color:" + (dis ? C.mute : C.ivory),
          go: function () {
            if (dis) { self.say("This story needs the submitter's GDPR acknowledgement before it can be accepted."); return; }
            if (r.at === "pub") { self.audit("Published story to " + s.storyDest, "Test Story"); self.say("Story published to the " + s.storyDest + "."); return; }
            self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.concat([{ id: Date.now(), kind: "Story", title: "Test Story", stage: "Pregnancy", aspect: "Stories & Experience", tier: "Experience", by: "Anonymous", when: "05/04/2026", words: "380", blurb: "A parent's own story, sent in from the public site.", at: r.at, gdpr: true, notes: [], dest: s.storyDest, reviewedBy: r.at === "pre" ? me.initials : "" }]); });
            self.say("Story sent to " + r.label + " · destination " + s.storyDest + ".");
          } };
      })
    });
    return out;
  }

  v5vals() {
    const self = this, s = this.state;
    const me = this.me();
    const canPublish = CAN_PUBLISH.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;
    const canApprove = CAN_APPROVE.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;
    const isEditor = s.role === "content_editor";
    const FORMATS = ["Article", "Guide", "Checklist", "Explainer"], CREATORS = ["KG", "LG", "SK"];
    ARTICLES.forEach(function (a, i) {
      if (a._v5) return;
      const cats = CATS.filter(function (c) { return c.stage === a.stage; });
      const cat = cats.length ? cats[i % cats.length] : null;
      a.category = a.category || (cat ? cat.name : "");
      a.topicList = a.topicList || (cat ? cat.topics.slice(i % 3, i % 3 + 2) : []);
      a.format = a.format || FORMATS[i % 4];
      if (a.cg == null) a.cg = i % 4 === 1;
      a.creator = a.creator || CREATORS[i % 3];
      if (a.medReviewed == null) a.medReviewed = a.status === "live" || i % 3 !== 0;
      a.reviewers = a.reviewers || (a.medReviewed ? ["AR"] : []);
      if (a.everLive == null) a.everLive = a.status !== "draft" || i % 7 === 3;
      if (a.published == null) a.published = a.status !== "draft" || i % 5 === 2;
      a.pubDate = a.pubDate || (a.published ? a.date : "");
      a._v5 = true;
    });
    const changedTitles = s.q.filter(function (x) { return x.at === "changes"; }).map(function (x) { return x.title; });
    const warnsOf = function (a) {
      const w = [];
      if (a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed" || !a.aspect) w.push({ key: "aspect", label: "Aspect unconfirmed" });
      if (a.warn === "Tier unconfirmed" || !a.tier) w.push({ key: "tier", label: "Missing evidence tier" });
      if (!a.medReviewed && (a.aspect === "Medical & Clinical" || a.tier === "clinical_adjacent" || a.tier === "scientific")) w.push({ key: "medical", label: "Missing medical review" });
      if (changedTitles.indexOf(a.title) !== -1) w.push({ key: "changes", label: "Requires changes" });
      return w;
    };
    const webTest = {
      on: function (a) { return a.status === "live"; },
      was: function (a) { return a.everLive && a.status !== "live"; },
      not: function (a) { return a.status !== "live"; },
      never: function (a) { return !a.everLive && a.status !== "live"; },
      pubOff: function (a) { return a.published && a.status !== "live"; },
      featOff: function (a) { return a.featured && a.status !== "live"; }
    };
    const webLabel = function (a) { return a.status === "live" ? "On the Web" : a.featured ? "Featured – Not on Site" : a.published ? "Published – Not on Site" : a.everLive ? "Was Live" : "Never on the Web"; };
    const tierOf = function (id) { return TIERS.filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0] || TIERS[4]; };
    const q = (s.artQ || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const inStatus = function (a, st) {
      if (st === "draft") return a.status === "draft";
      if (st === "live") return a.status === "live";
      if (st === "off_site") return a.status === "off_site";
      if (st === "featured") return !!a.featured;
      if (st === "published") return !!a.published;
      if (st === "was") return webTest.was(a);
      if (st === "attention") return warnsOf(a).length > 0;
      return true;
    };
    const list = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) {
      if (!inStatus(a, s.artStatus)) return false;
      if (s.artStatus === "attention" && s.attnSub !== "all" && !warnsOf(a).some(function (w) { return w.key === s.attnSub; })) return false;
      if (s.artStage !== "all" && a.stage !== s.artStage) return false;
      if (s.artCat !== "all" && a.category !== s.artCat) return false;
      if (s.artTopic !== "all" && a.topicList.indexOf(s.artTopic) === -1) return false;
      if (s.artFormat !== "all" && a.format !== s.artFormat) return false;
      if (s.artCg === "yes" && !a.cg) return false;
      if (s.artCg === "no" && a.cg) return false;
      if (s.artTier !== "all" && a.tier !== s.artTier) return false;
      if (s.artAspect !== "all" && a.aspect !== s.artAspect) return false;
      if (s.artFeat === "yes" && !a.featured) return false;
      if (s.artFeat === "no" && a.featured) return false;
      if (s.artWeb !== "all" && !webTest[s.artWeb](a)) return false;
      if (q && (a.title + " " + a.blurb + " " + a.category + " " + a.topicList.join(" ") + " " + a.author).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) === -1) return false;
      return true;
    });
    const rank = function (a) { return warnsOf(a).length ? 0 : a.status === "draft" ? 1 : 2; };
    const dt = function (d) { const p = (d || "").split("/"); return p.length === 3 ? new Date(+p[2], +p[1] - 1, +p[0]).getTime() : 0; };
    const sorted = list.slice().sort(function (x, y) {
      if (s.artSort === "title") return x.title.localeCompare(y.title);
      if (s.artSort === "newest") return dt(y.date) - dt(x.date);
      if (s.artSort === "oldest") return dt(x.date) - dt(y.date);
      return rank(x) - rank(y);
    });
    const btn = function (kind, disabled) {
      const base = "border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;";
      if (disabled) return base + "background:#fff;color:" + C.mute + ";border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";cursor:not-allowed";
      if (kind === "primary") return base + "background:" + C.cta + ";color:" + C.ivory + ";border:none;cursor:pointer";
      if (kind === "go") return base + "background:" + C.sage700 + ";color:" + C.ivory + ";border:none;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600";
      if (kind === "danger") return base + "background:none;color:" + C.clayM + ";border:1px solid " + C.clayL + ";cursor:pointer;margin-left:auto";
      return base + "background:#fff;color:" + C.char + ";border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";cursor:pointer";
    };
    const submitToReview = function (a) {
      self.setQ(function (qq) {
        return [{ id: Date.now(), kind: "Article", title: a.title, stage: a.stage, aspect: a.aspect, tier: tierOf(a.tier).label, by: me.name, when: "just now",
          words: a.words || "—", blurb: a.blurb, at: "review", notes: [] }].concat(qq.filter(function (x) { return x.title !== a.title; }));
      }, { open: null });
      self.say("Submitted. It is now in For review.");
    };
    const rows = sorted.map(function (a) {
      const t = tierOf(a.tier), st = STATUS_STYLE[a.status] || STATUS_STYLE.draft, w = warnsOf(a), open = s.open === a.title;
      const locked = isEditor && (a.status === "live" || a.published);
      const blocking = w.filter(function (x) { return x.key !== "medical"; });
      const acts = [];
      if (self.can("new") && !locked) acts.push({ label: "Edit", kind: "primary", run: function () {
        const asp = ASPECTS.filter(function (o) { return o.label === a.aspect; })[0];
        self.setState({ screen: "new", open: null, query: "", accountOpen: false, editingTitle: a.title, draftTitle: a.title, draftStage: a.stage, draftAspect: asp ? asp.value : "",
          draftTier: a.tier, draftBody: a.blurb, draftBlurb: a.blurb, draftInitials: "", draftCat: a.category, draftTopicSel: a.topicList.slice(), draftCg: !!a.cg, draftFormat: a.format, draftKeywords: a.keywords || "", draftFeat: !!a.featured, draftPreamble: a.preamble || "" });
      } });
      else acts.push({ label: "Read full article", kind: "ghost", title: locked ? "Published articles can only be edited by a Supervisor or Tech." : "",
        run: function () { self.setState({ preview: { title: a.title, blurb: a.blurb, stage: a.stage, category: a.category }, previewMode: "public" }); } });
      acts.push({ label: "Preview", kind: "ghost", run: function () { self.setState({ preview: { title: a.title, blurb: a.blurb, stage: a.stage, category: a.category }, previewMode: "public" }); } });
      if (a.status === "draft" && self.can("new") && !canPublish) acts.push({ label: "Submit for review", kind: "go", disabled: blocking.length > 0,
        title: blocking.length ? "Resolve: " + blocking.map(function (x) { return x.label.toLowerCase(); }).join(", ") + "." : "", run: function () { submitToReview(a); } });
      if (a.status !== "live" && canPublish) acts.push({ label: "Publish", kind: "go", disabled: w.length > 0,
        title: w.length ? "Resolve: " + w.map(function (x) { return x.label.toLowerCase(); }).join(", ") + "." : "",
        run: function () { a.status = "live"; a.everLive = true; a.published = true; a.pubDate = a.pubDate || "13/10/2026"; self.audit("Published", a.title); self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” is now live."); } });
      if (a.status === "live" && canPublish) acts.push({ label: "Take off site", kind: "ghost",
        run: function () { a.status = "off_site"; self.audit("Took off site", a.title); self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” has been taken off the site."); } });
      if (canPublish) acts.push({ label: a.featured ? "Unfeature" : "Feature", kind: "ghost",
        run: function () { a.featured = !a.featured; self.audit(a.featured ? "Featured" : "Unfeatured", a.title); self.setState({ open: null }); self.say(a.featured ? "Featured." : "No longer featured."); } });
      if (canPublish) acts.push({ label: "Delete", kind: "danger", run: function () {
        self.askConfirm({ title: "Delete this article?", body: "“" + a.title + "” and its revision history will be removed. This cannot be undone.", label: "Delete article",
          run: function () { const ix = ARTICLES.indexOf(a); if (ix !== -1) ARTICLES.splice(ix, 1); self.audit("Deleted article", a.title); self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” deleted."); } });
      } });
      const topics = a.topicList.length ? a.topicList.map(function (x, i) { return i === 0 ? x + " (primary)" : x; }).join(", ") : "None assigned";
      return {
        title: a.title, stage: a.stage, aspect: a.aspect || "—", tier: t.label, status: st.label, stageColor: STAGE_COLOR[a.stage],
        warn: w.length ? w[0].label + (w.length > 1 ? " +" + (w.length - 1) : "") : "", featured: a.featured, blurb: a.blurb, open: open,
        tierStyle: self.pill(t.color, t.bg), statusStyle: self.pill(st.color, st.bg),
        chevStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:transform .18s ease;transform:rotate(" + (open ? "180deg" : "0deg") + ")",
        rowStyle: "min-width:968px;border-bottom:1px solid " + C.linen + ";background:" + (open ? C.ivory : "#fff"),
        toggle: function () { self.setState({ open: open ? null : a.title }); },
        warnings: w.map(function (x) { return { label: x.label }; }), hasWarnings: w.length > 0,
        details: [
          ["Category", a.category || "—"], ["Topics", topics], ["Format", a.format], ["Connection & Growth", a.cg ? "Yes" : "No"],
          ["Featured", a.featured ? "Yes" : "No"], ["Web status", webLabel(a)], ["Author", a.author], ["Created by", a.creator],
          ["Reviewed by", a.reviewers.length ? a.reviewers.join(", ") : "Not yet reviewed"], ["Medically reviewed", a.medReviewed ? "Yes" : "Not reviewed"],
          ["Published", a.pubDate || "—"], ["Updated", a.date]
        ].map(function (d) { return { label: d[0], value: d[1] }; }),
        actions: acts.map(function (x) { return { label: x.label, style: btn(x.kind, x.disabled), disabled: !!x.disabled, title: x.title || "", run: x.run || function () { self.say(x.msg); } }; })
      };
    });
    const pg = this.pgr(rows, "pgArt", 8);
    const count = function (st) { return ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return inStatus(a, st); }).length; };
    const statDefs = [
      { id: "attention", label: "Needs attention", color: C.clayM }, { id: "draft", label: "Drafts", color: C.charL },
      { id: "published", label: "Published", color: C.sage900 }, { id: "featured", label: "Featured", color: C.clay },
      { id: "live", label: "On the web", color: C.sage700 }, { id: "was", label: "Was live on the web", color: C.clayD },
      { id: "all", label: "All", color: C.charD }
    ];
    const subs = [["all", "All attention"], ["aspect", "Aspect unconfirmed"], ["tier", "Missing evidence tier"], ["medical", "Missing medical review"], ["changes", "Requires changes"]];
    const mk = function (key, all, opts) {
      return { value: s[key], options: [{ value: "all", label: all }].concat(opts),
        onChange: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; const o = { open: null, pgArt: 1 }; o[key] = v; if (key === "artStage") { o.artCat = "all"; o.artTopic = "all"; } if (key === "artCat") o.artTopic = "all"; self.setState(o); } };
    };
    const catOpts = CATS.filter(function (c) { return s.artStage === "all" || c.stage === s.artStage; }).map(function (c) { return { value: c.name, label: s.artStage === "all" ? c.stage + " · " + c.name : c.name }; });
    const filters = [
      mk("artStage", "All stages", Object.keys(STAGE_COLOR).map(function (k) { return { value: k, label: k }; })),
      mk("artCat", "All categories", catOpts),
      mk("artTopic", "All topics", CATS.filter(function (c) { return (s.artStage === "all" || c.stage === s.artStage) && (s.artCat === "all" || c.name === s.artCat); })
        .reduce(function (acc, c) { return acc.concat(c.topics); }, []).filter(function (x, i, arr) { return arr.indexOf(x) === i; }).sort().map(function (x) { return { value: x, label: x }; })),
      mk("artFormat", "All formats", ["Article", "Guide", "Checklist", "Explainer", "Video"].map(function (x) { return { value: x, label: x }; })),
      mk("artCg", "C&G and not", [{ value: "yes", label: "Connection & Growth" }, { value: "no", label: "Not C&G" }]),
      mk("artTier", "All evidence tiers", TIERS.filter(function (t) { return t.id !== "ai_created"; }).map(function (t) { return { value: t.id, label: t.label }; })),
      mk("artAspect", "All aspects", ASPECTS.map(function (a) { return { value: a.label, label: a.label }; })),
      mk("artFeat", "Featured or not", [{ value: "yes", label: "Featured" }, { value: "no", label: "Not featured" }]),
      mk("artWeb", "All (web status)", [{ value: "on", label: "On the Web" }, { value: "was", label: "Was Live" }, { value: "not", label: "Not on Web" }, { value: "never", label: "Never on the Web" }, { value: "pubOff", label: "Published – Not on Site" }, { value: "featOff", label: "Featured – Not on Site" }]),
      { value: s.artSort, options: [{ value: "default", label: "Sort: attention first" }, { value: "newest", label: "Sort: newest" }, { value: "oldest", label: "Sort: oldest" }, { value: "title", label: "Sort: title A–Z" }],
        onChange: function (e) { self.setState({ artSort: e.target.value, pgArt: 1 }); } }
    ];
    const qCard = function (x) {
      const t = TIERS.filter(function (y) { return y.label === x.tier; })[0] || TIERS[0];
      const noteOpen = s.qNoteOpen === x.id;
      return Object.assign({}, x, {
        stageColor: STAGE_COLOR[x.stage], stageTint: STAGE_TINT[x.stage], tierStyle: self.pill(t.color, t.bg),
        isStory: x.kind === "Story", kindStyle: self.pill(x.kind === "Story" ? C.clayD : C.sage900, x.kind === "Story" ? "#F7EFE9" : C.sageSoft),
        gdprText: x.gdpr ? "GDPR acknowledgement recorded" : "GDPR acknowledgement missing — cannot be accepted",
        gdprColor: x.gdpr ? C.sage700 : C.clayM, gdprBlock: x.kind === "Story" && !x.gdpr,
        approvedText: x.reviewedBy ? "Approved by " + x.reviewedBy : "",
        hasNotes: x.notes.length > 0, notesList: x.notes.map(function (n) { return { text: n.text, meta: n.by + " · " + n.when }; }),
        noteOpen: noteOpen, noteVal: s.qNote[x.id] || "",
        onNote: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function (st) { const o = Object.assign({}, st.qNote); o[x.id] = v; return { qNote: o }; }); },
        openNote: function () { self.setState({ qNoteOpen: noteOpen ? null : x.id }); },
        readFull: function () { self.setState({ preview: { title: x.title, blurb: x.blurb, stage: x.stage, category: x.kind === "Story" ? "Story" : x.aspect }, previewMode: "public" }); },
        cancelNote: function () { self.setState({ qNoteOpen: null }); }
      });
    };
    const tabbed = function (at, tab) { return s.q.filter(function (x) { return x.at === at && (tab === "All" || (tab === "Stories" ? x.kind === "Story" : x.kind === "Article")); }); };
    const tabs = function (at, key) {
      return ["All", "Articles", "Stories"].map(function (l) {
        const on = s[key] === l; const n = tabbed(at, l).length;
        return { label: l + " (" + n + ")", go: function () { self.set(key, l); },
          style: "padding:8px 14px;border:none;border-bottom:2px solid " + (on ? C.sage700 : "transparent") + ";background:none;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:" + (on ? "600" : "500") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage700 : C.charL) };
      });
    };
    const reviewList = tabbed("review", s.reviewTab).map(function (x) {
      const c = qCard(x);
      return Object.assign(c, {
        canAct: canApprove, readOnly: !canApprove,
        approve: function () {
          if (c.gdprBlock) { self.say("A story needs its GDPR acknowledgement before it can be approved."); return; }
          self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.map(function (y) { return y.id === x.id ? Object.assign({}, y, { at: "pre", reviewedBy: me.initials, notes: y.notes.concat([{ by: me.initials, text: "Approved for Pre-Published.", when: "just now" }]) }) : y; }); }, { qNoteOpen: null });
          self.audit("Approved for Pre-Published", x.title);
          self.say("Approved. “" + x.title + "” is now in Pre-Published.");
        },
        sendBack: function () {
          const note = (s.qNote[x.id] || "").trim();
          if (!note) { self.say("Add a note so the writer knows what to change."); return; }
          self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.map(function (y) { return y.id === x.id ? Object.assign({}, y, { at: "changes", notes: y.notes.concat([{ by: me.initials, text: note, when: "just now" }]) }) : y; }); }, { qNoteOpen: null });
          self.audit("Marked requires changes", x.title);
          self.say("Returned to " + x.by + " with your note.");
        }
      });
    });
    const preList = tabbed("pre", s.preTab).map(function (x) {
      const c = qCard(x);
      return Object.assign(c, {
        canAct: canPublish, readOnly: !canPublish,
        publish: function () {
          self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.filter(function (y) { return y.id !== x.id; }); });
          const art = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.title === x.title; })[0];
          if (art) { art.status = "live"; art.everLive = true; art.published = true; art.pubDate = "13/10/2026"; art.medReviewed = true; art.reviewers = [x.reviewedBy || me.initials]; }
          self.audit("Published", x.title);
          self.say("“" + x.title + "” is published and live.");
        },
        sendBack: function () {
          const note = (s.qNote[x.id] || "").trim();
          if (!note) { self.say("Add a note for the reviewer."); return; }
          self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.map(function (y) { return y.id === x.id ? Object.assign({}, y, { at: "review", reviewedBy: "", notes: y.notes.concat([{ by: me.initials, text: note, when: "just now" }]) }) : y; }); }, { qNoteOpen: null });
          self.audit("Returned to For review", x.title);
          self.say("Sent back to For review.");
        }
      });
    });
    const changes = s.q.filter(function (x) { return x.at === "changes"; }).map(function (x) {
      const last = x.notes[x.notes.length - 1] || { text: "", by: "", when: "" };
      return { title: x.title, note: last.text, meta: "Returned by " + last.by + " · " + last.when, stageColor: STAGE_COLOR[x.stage],
        resubmit: function () { self.setQ(function (qq) { return qq.map(function (y) { return y.id === x.id ? Object.assign({}, y, { at: "review", when: "just now" }) : y; }); }); self.say("Resubmitted. It is back in For review."); } };
    });
    return {
      artStats: statDefs.map(function (d) {
        const on = s.artStatus === d.id;
        return { label: d.label, n: String(count(d.id)), color: d.color,
          style: "display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:9999px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? "#fff" : "transparent"),
          go: function () { self.setState({ artStatus: d.id, attnSub: "all", open: null, pgArt: 1 }); } };
      }),
      attnOn: s.artStatus === "attention",
      attnSubs: subs.map(function (x) {
        const on = s.attnSub === x[0];
        const n = x[0] === "all" ? count("attention") : ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return warnsOf(a).some(function (w) { return w.key === x[0]; }); }).length;
        return { label: x[1], n: String(n), go: function () { self.setState({ attnSub: x[0], pgArt: 1, open: null }); },
          style: "display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:9999px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;border:1px solid " + (on ? C.clayM : C.clayL) + ";background:" + (on ? C.clayM : "#fff") + ";color:" + (on ? C.ivory : C.clayM) };
      }),
      artFilters: filters, artRows: pg.items, artPager: pg.bar,
      artShowing: sorted.length + " of " + ARTICLES.length + " articles",
      artFiltered: s.artStatus !== "all" || s.artStage !== "all" || s.artTier !== "all" || s.artAspect !== "all" || s.artCat !== "all" || s.artTopic !== "all" || s.artFormat !== "all" || s.artCg !== "all" || s.artFeat !== "all" || s.artWeb !== "all" || !!s.artQ,
      artEmpty: sorted.length === 0,
      clearArt: function () { self.setState({ artStatus: "all", artStage: "all", artTier: "all", artAspect: "all", artCat: "all", artTopic: "all", artFormat: "all", artCg: "all", artFeat: "all", artWeb: "all", artSort: "default", attnSub: "all", artQ: "", open: null, pgArt: 1 }); },
      changesItems: changes, hasChanges: changes.length > 0 && self.can("new"),
      reviewTabs: tabs("review", "reviewTab"), preTabs: tabs("pre", "preTab"),
      reviewItems: reviewList, noReview: reviewList.length === 0,
      preItems: preList, noPre: preList.length === 0, hasPre: preList.length > 0,
      preCountText: preList.length + " waiting on final review",
      signAs: "Signing as " + me.initials,
      canPre: self.can("pre")
    };
  }

  renderVals() {
    const s = this.state;
    const p = this.props;
    const self = this;
    const rowH = p.density === "comfortable" ? 60 : 48;
    const showBadges = p.showBadges !== false;
    const dark = p.sidebarTone !== "light";
    const roleObj = ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r.id === s.role; })[0] || ROLES[0];
    const me = this.me();
    const canApprove = CAN_APPROVE.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;
    const canPublish = CAN_PUBLISH.indexOf(s.role) !== -1;

    const counts = { draft: 0, live: 0, off_site: 0, featured: 0, warn: 0 };
    ARTICLES.forEach(function (a) {
      counts[a.status] = (counts[a.status] || 0) + 1;
      if (a.featured) counts.featured++;
      if (a.warn) counts.warn++;
    });

    const badges = {
      morpheus: "9+",
      safety: s.flags.filter(function (x) { return !x.done; }).length ? String(s.flags.filter(function (x) { return !x.done; }).length) : "",
      pre: s.pre.length ? String(s.pre.length) : "",
      review: s.review.length ? String(s.review.length) : "",
      stories: s.gdpr.length === 4 ? "" : "1"
    };
    const badgeColors = { morpheus: C.moira, pre: C.clayM, review: C.slate, stories: C.clayD, safety: "#8C3820" };

    const rail = !!s.rail && !s.narrow;
    const navVisible = NAV.filter(function (n) { return n.group || self.can(n.key); });
    const navItems = navVisible.filter(function (n, i) {
      if (!n.group) return true;
      const next = navVisible[i + 1];
      return !!next && !next.group;
    }).map(function (n) {
      if (n.group) return {
        isGroupLabel: !rail, isGroupRule: rail, isLink: false, label: n.group,
        wrapStyle: rail ? "padding:11px 0" : "padding:16px 0 7px"
      };
      const on = s.screen === n.id || (n.id === "articles" && s.screen === "new");
      const badge = showBadges && n.badgeKey ? badges[n.badgeKey] : "";
      return {
        isGroupLabel: false, isGroupRule: false, isLink: true, label: n.postBeta && !rail ? n.label + " \u00b7 post-beta" : n.label, icon: n.icon,
        wrapStyle: "padding:1px 0", showLabel: !rail,
        badge: rail ? "" : badge, dot: rail && !!badge,
        dotStyle: "position:absolute;top:-3px;right:-4px;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:9999px;border:1.5px solid " + (dark ? C.cta : C.linen) + ";background:" + (badgeColors[n.badgeKey] || C.clayM),
        badgeStyle: "margin-left:auto;flex:none;min-width:20px;height:19px;padding:0 6px;border-radius:9999px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:" + C.ivory + ";background:" + (badgeColors[n.badgeKey] || C.clayM),
        style: "width:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;border:none;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;text-align:left;font-size:13.5px;padding:" +
          (rail ? "10px 0;justify-content:center" : "9px 12px") +
