          ";font-weight:" + (on ? "600" : "500") +
          ";color:" + (on ? C.ivory : (dark ? "rgba(255,255,255,.9)" : C.charL)) +
          ";background:" + (on ? (dark ? C.ctaHover : C.sage700) : "transparent"),
        go: function () { self.go(n.id); }
      };
    });

    const allowed = this.can(s.screen);

    // ---- global search
    const q = s.query.trim().toLowerCase();
    let results = [];
    if (q.length > 1) {
      const tagBase = "flex:none;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;";
      if (self.can("articles")) ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.title.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1; }).slice(0, 4).forEach(function (a) {
        results.push({
          group: "Article", label: a.title, tagStyle: tagBase + "background:" + STAGE_TINT[a.stage] + ";color:" + STAGE_COLOR[a.stage],
          go: function () { self.setState({ screen: "articles", query: "", artQ: a.title, artStatus: "all", artStage: "all", artTier: "all", artAspect: "all", open: null, pgArt: 1 }); }
        });
      });
      if (self.can("users")) {
        USERS.filter(function (u) { return s.deletedUsers.indexOf(u.name) === -1 && (u.name + " " + u.email).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1; }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (u) {
          results.push({
            group: "Person", label: u.name + " · " + u.email, tagStyle: tagBase + "background:" + C.sage50 + ";color:" + C.sage700,
            go: function () { self.setState({ screen: "users", query: "", userQ: u.name, pgUsers: 1 }); }
          });
        });
      }
      if (self.can("docs")) {
        DOCS.filter(function (d) {
          const lib = s.libs.filter(function (x) { return x.name === (s.docLibMap[d[0]] || d[2]); })[0];
          return d[0].toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1 && s.docDel.indexOf(d[0]) === -1 && (!lib || lib.roles.indexOf(s.role) !== -1);
        }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (d) {
          results.push({
            group: "Document", label: d[0] + "." + d[1], tagStyle: tagBase + "background:" + C.linen + ";color:" + C.charL,
            go: function () { self.setState({ screen: "docs", query: "", docQ: d[0], docLib: "All", pgDocs: 1 }); }
          });
        });
      }
    }

    // ---- articles
    const artQ = s.artQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    const filtered = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) {
      if (s.artStatus === "draft" && a.status !== "draft") return false;
      if (s.artStatus === "live" && a.status !== "live") return false;
      if (s.artStatus === "featured" && !a.featured) return false;
      if (s.artStatus === "attention" && !a.warn) return false;
      if (s.artStage !== "all" && a.stage !== s.artStage) return false;
      if (s.artTier !== "all" && a.tier !== s.artTier) return false;
      if (s.artAspect !== "all" && a.aspect !== s.artAspect) return false;
      if (artQ && a.title.toLowerCase().indexOf(artQ) === -1) return false;
      return true;
    });

    const actBtn = function (kind, disabled) {
      if (disabled) return "background:#fff;color:" + C.mute + ";border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:not-allowed";
      if (kind === "primary") return "background:" + C.cta + ";color:" + C.ivory + ";border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer";
      if (kind === "go") return "background:" + C.sage700 + ";color:" + C.ivory + ";border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer";
      if (kind === "danger") return "background:none;color:" + C.clayM + ";border:1px solid " + C.clayL + ";border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;margin-left:auto";
      return "background:#fff;color:" + C.char + ";border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";border-radius:9999px;padding:7px 15px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer";
    };

    const artRows = filtered.map(function (a, i) {
      const tier = TIERS.filter(function (t) { return t.id === a.tier; })[0] || TIERS[4];
      const st = STATUS_STYLE[a.status];
      const open = s.open === i;
      const actions = [];
      if (self.can("new")) actions.push({
        label: "Edit", kind: "primary",
        run: function () {
          const asp = ASPECTS.find(function (o) { return o.label === a.aspect; });
          self.setState({
            screen: "new", open: null, query: "", accountOpen: false,
            draftTitle: a.title, draftStage: a.stage, draftAspect: asp ? asp.value : "", draftTier: a.tier,
            draftBody: a.blurb, draftInitials: ""
          });
        }
      });
      else actions.push({ label: "Read", kind: "ghost", msg: "Opening “" + a.title + "” read-only." });
      actions.push({ label: "Preview on site", kind: "ghost", msg: "Preview opened in a new tab." });
      if (a.status === "draft" && self.can("new") && !canPublish) actions.push({
        label: "Submit for approval", kind: "go", disabled: !!a.warn,
        title: a.warn ? "Confirm the " + a.warn.replace(" unconfirmed", "").toLowerCase() + " before submitting." : "",
        msg: "Submitted. It is now awaiting approval."
      });
      if (a.status === "draft" && canPublish) actions.push({
        label: "Publish", kind: "go", disabled: !!a.warn,
        title: a.warn ? "Confirm the " + a.warn.replace(" unconfirmed", "").toLowerCase() + " before publishing." : "",
        run: function () { a.status = "live"; self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” is now live."); }
      });
      if (a.status === "pending" && canPublish) actions.push({
        label: "Publish", kind: "go",
        run: function () { a.status = "live"; self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” is now live."); }
      });
      if (a.status === "live" && canPublish) actions.push({
        label: "Take off site", kind: "ghost",
        run: function () { a.status = "off_site"; self.setState({ open: null }); self.say("“" + a.title + "” has been taken off the site."); }
      });
      if (s.role === "super_user") actions.push({
        label: "Delete", kind: "danger",
        run: function () {
          self.askConfirm({
            title: "Delete this article?",
            body: "“" + a.title + "” and its revision history will be removed. This cannot be undone.",
            label: "Delete article",
            run: function () {
              const ix = ARTICLES.indexOf(a);
              if (ix !== -1) ARTICLES.splice(ix, 1);
              const next = Object.assign({}, s.featAssign);
              Object.keys(next).forEach(function (k) { if (next[k] && next[k].title === a.title) delete next[k]; });
              self.setState({ open: null, featAssign: next });
              self.say("“" + a.title + "” deleted.");
            }
          });
        }
      });

      return {
        title: a.title, stage: a.stage, aspect: a.aspect, tier: tier.label, status: st.label,
        stageColor: STAGE_COLOR[a.stage], warn: a.warn, featured: a.featured,
        blurb: a.blurb, author: a.author, date: a.date, topics: a.topics, open: open,
        tierStyle: self.pill(tier.color, tier.bg),
        statusStyle: self.pill(st.color, st.bg),
        chevStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:transform .18s ease;transform:rotate(" + (open ? "180deg" : "0deg") + ")",
        rowStyle: "min-width:968px;border-bottom:1px solid " + C.linen + ";background:" + (open ? C.ivory : "#fff"),
        toggle: function () { self.setState({ open: open ? null : i }); },
        actions: actions.map(function (act) {
          return {
            label: act.label, style: actBtn(act.kind, act.disabled), disabled: !!act.disabled, title: act.title || "",
            run: act.run || function () { self.say(act.msg); }
          };
        })
      };
    });

    const statDefs = [
      { id: "all", label: "All", n: ARTICLES.length, color: C.charD },
      { id: "draft", label: "Drafts", n: counts.draft, color: C.charL },
      { id: "live", label: "Live", n: counts.live, color: C.sage700 },
      { id: "featured", label: "Featured", n: counts.featured, color: C.clay },
      { id: "attention", label: "Needs attention", n: counts.warn, color: C.clayM }
    ];
    const artStats = statDefs.map(function (d) {
      const on = s.artStatus === d.id;
      return {
        label: d.label, n: d.n, color: d.color,
        style: "display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:9999px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " +
          (on ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? "#fff" : "transparent"),
        go: function () { self.setState({ artStatus: d.id, open: null, pgArt: 1 }); }
      };
    });

    const mkFilter = function (key, all, opts) {
      return {
        value: s[key], options: [{ value: "all", label: all }].concat(opts),
        onChange: function (e) { const v = e.target.value; self.setState(function () { const o = {}; o[key] = v; o.open = null; o.pgArt = 1; return o; }); }
      };
    };
    const artFilters = [
      mkFilter("artStage", "All stages", Object.keys(STAGE_COLOR).map(function (k) { return { value: k, label: k }; })),
      mkFilter("artTier", "All evidence tiers", TIERS.map(function (t) { return { value: t.id, label: t.label }; })),
      mkFilter("artAspect", "All aspects", ASPECTS.map(function (a) { return { value: a.label, label: a.label }; }))
    ];
    const artFiltered = s.artStatus !== "all" || s.artStage !== "all" || s.artTier !== "all" || s.artAspect !== "all" || !!s.artQ;

    // ---- dashboard attention, permission-filtered
    const attentionAll = [
      { key: "review", count: String(s.review.length), label: "For review", note: "Waiting on a reviewer to approve or return.", color: C.slateInk, screen: "review" },
      { key: "pre", count: String(s.pre.length), label: "Pre-Published", note: "Approved by a reviewer, waiting on final review.", color: C.clayM, screen: "pre" },
      { key: "articles", count: String(counts.warn), label: "Unconfirmed aspect", note: "Drafts that cannot be published until classified.", color: C.clayD, screen: "articles" },
      { key: "stories", count: "1", label: "Story submission", note: "Incomplete — five fields still needed.", color: C.before, screen: "stories" },
      { key: "morpheus", count: "9+", label: "Unreviewed keywords", note: "Search terms nobody has triaged yet.", color: C.moira, screen: "morpheus" }
    ].filter(function (a) { return self.can(a.key) && a.count !== "0"; });

    const attention = attentionAll.map(function (a) {
      return {
        count: a.count, label: a.label, note: a.note, color: a.color,
        go: function () { self.go(a.screen); }
      };
    });

    const pipeStyle = "text-align:left;background:none;border:none;padding:0;flex:1;cursor:pointer";
    const pipeline = [
      { stage: "Drafts", n: String(counts.draft), color: C.charL, arrow: true, screen: "articles" },
      { stage: "For review", n: String(s.review.length), color: C.slateInk, arrow: true, screen: "review" },
      { stage: "Pre-Published", n: String(s.pre.length), color: C.clayM, arrow: true, screen: "pre" },
      { stage: "Live", n: String(counts.live), color: C.sage700, arrow: false, screen: "articles" }
    ].map(function (d) {
      return Object.assign({}, d, { noArrow: !d.arrow, style: pipeStyle, go: function () { if (self.can(d.screen)) self.go(d.screen); else self.say("Your role cannot open that queue."); } });
    });

    // ---- morpheus
    const mSteps = [
      { n: 1, label: "Find a trend", note: "What parents are searching" },
      { n: 2, label: "Gather sources", note: "Pick what to draw on" },
      { n: 3, label: "Voice and generate", note: "Lands as an editable draft" }
    ].map(function (d) {
      const on = s.mStep === d.n;
      const done = s.mStep > d.n;
      const reachable = d.n <= s.mMax;
      return {
        n: done ? "✓" : String(d.n), label: d.label, note: d.note,
        style: "flex:1;min-width:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:12px 14px;border:none;border-radius:10px;text-align:left;cursor:" + (reachable ? "pointer" : "not-allowed") +
          ";opacity:" + (reachable ? "1" : ".45") + ";background:" + (on ? C.sageSoft : "transparent") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage900 : C.charL),
        numStyle: "width:24px;height:24px;flex:none;border-radius:9999px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;background:" +
          (on ? C.moira : done ? C.sage200 : C.linen) + ";color:" + (on ? C.ivory : done ? C.sage900 : C.charL),
        go: function () { if (reachable) self.set("mStep", d.n); else self.say("Finish step " + s.mMax + " first."); }
      };
    });

    const trendGroups = TREND_DATA.map(function (g) {
      return {
        name: g.name, color: g.color,
        trends: g.trends.map(function (t) {
          const on = s.trends.indexOf(t[0]) !== -1;
          return {
            text: t[0], vol: t[1], on: on, boxStyle: self.box(on, C.moira),
            style: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;border:1px solid " +
              (on ? C.sage200 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sageSoft : "#fff") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage900 : C.char),
            pick: function () {
              self.setState(function (st) {
                const arr = st.trends.slice();
                const i = arr.indexOf(t[0]);
                if (i === -1) arr.push(t[0]); else arr.splice(i, 1);
                return { trends: arr };
              });
            }
          };
        })
      };
    });

    const sources = SRC.map(function (src, i) {
      const on = s.sources.indexOf(i) !== -1;
      return {
        title: src[0], meta: src[1], on: on, boxStyle: self.box(on, C.moira),
        style: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:13px 18px;border:none;border-bottom:1px solid " + C.linen + ";cursor:pointer;background:" + (on ? C.ivory : "#fff"),
        toggle: function () {
          self.setState(function (st) {
            const arr = st.sources.slice();
            const k = arr.indexOf(i);
            if (k === -1) arr.push(i); else arr.splice(k, 1);
            return { sources: arr };
          });
        }
      };
    });

    const canNext = s.mStep === 1 ? s.trends.length > 0 : s.sources.length > 0;
    const aiPrompts = AI_PROMPTS.map(function (t, i) {
      const on = s.aiPick === i;
      return {
        text: t, style: "text-align:left;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.45;border:1px solid " +
          (on ? C.moira : C.sage200) + ";background:" + (on ? "#fff" : "rgba(255,255,255,.55)") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage900 : "#3A5540") + ";font-weight:" + (on ? "600" : "400"),
        pick: function () { self.set("aiPick", i); }
      };
    });

    // ---- new article
    const stageChips = Object.keys(STAGE_COLOR).map(function (k) {
      const on = s.draftStage === k;
      return { label: k, style: self.chip(on, STAGE_COLOR[k]), pick: function () { self.set("draftStage", on ? "" : k); } };
    });
    const tierChips = TIERS.map(function (t) {
      const on = s.draftTier === t.id;
      return { label: t.label, style: self.chip(on, t.color), pick: function () { self.set("draftTier", on ? "" : t.id); } };
    });
    const classDone = (s.draftStage ? 1 : 0) + (s.draftAspect ? 1 : 0) + (s.draftTier ? 1 : 0);
    const ready = classDone === 3 && !!s.draftTitle && !!s.draftInitials;
    const missing = [];
    if (!s.draftTitle) missing.push("title");
    if (!s.draftStage) missing.push("stage");
    if (!s.draftAspect) missing.push("aspect");
    if (!s.draftTier) missing.push("evidence tier");
    if (!s.draftInitials) missing.push("initials");

    // ---- stories
    const gdprDone = s.gdpr.length === 4;
    const gdprPanel = s.storiesLive
      ? "background:" + C.sageSoft + ";border:1px solid " + C.sage200 + ";border-radius:14px;padding:20px 22px"
      : "background:#FDF8EC;border:1px solid " + C.clayL + ";border-radius:14px;padding:20px 22px";
    const gdprBtnOn = gdprDone && !s.storiesLive;

    const storyTabs = ["All", "Submitted", "Under review", "Approved", "Published", "Rejected"].map(function (l) {

              const arr = st.suspended.slice();
              const i = arr.indexOf(u.name);
              if (i === -1) arr.push(u.name); else arr.splice(i, 1);
              return { suspended: arr };
            });

            self.say(susp ? u.name + " restored." : u.name + " suspended. They can no longer sign in.");
          };

            title: "Suspend this account?",

            run: flip
          });

        title: a.title, blurb: a.blurb, stage: a.stage, open: open,
        rowStyle: "background:#fff;border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";border-radius:11px;overflow:hidden",
        headStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:13px 15px;border:none;background:" + (open ? C.ivory : "#fff") + ";cursor:pointer",
        titleStyle: "font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;color:" + C.charD + ";overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap",
        stagePillStyle: "flex:none;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:" + col + ";background:" + col + "1F",
        chevStyle: "margin-left:auto;flex:none;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:" + C.mute + ";transform:rotate(" + (open ? "180deg" : "0deg") + ");transition:transform .16s",
        statusLabel: st.label,
        statusStyle: "flex:none;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:" + st.color + ";background:" + st.bg,
        meta: a.aspect + " · " + tier.label + " · updated " + a.date,
        toggle: function () { self.setState({ idxOpen: open ? null : a.title }); }
      };
    });

    const featFilled = Object.keys(s.featAssign).filter(function (k) { return !!s.featAssign[k]; }).length;
    const featGroups = ["Before Pregnancy", "Pregnancy", "Birth", "First Years"].map(function (st) {
      const col = STAGE_COLOR[st] || C.charL;
      const slots = FEAT_SLOTS.filter(function (sl) { return sl.stage === st; });
      const filled = slots.filter(function (sl) { return !!s.featAssign[sl.key]; }).length;
      return {
        label: st, filledLabel: filled + "/" + slots.length + " filled",
        dotStyle: "width:9px;height:9px;flex:none;border-radius:9999px;background:" + col,
        slots: slots.map(function (sl) {
          const as = s.featAssign[sl.key];
          const art = as ? ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.title === as.title; })[0] : null;
          return {
            cat: sl.cat, filled: !!as, empty: !as,
            title: as ? as.title : "",
            assigned: as ? "Assigned " + as.date : "",
            stageBadge: art ? art.stage : "",
            stageBadgeStyle: "flex:none;padding:2px 9px;border-radius:9999px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;color:" + C.ivory + ";background:" + col,

              ? "background:#fff;border:1px solid " + C.sand + ";border-radius:13px;padding:15px 16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px"
              : "background:" + C.ivory + ";border:1px dashed " + C.sand + ";border-radius:13px;padding:15px 16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px",

              self.setState({
                assignDlg: {
                  key: sl.key, cat: sl.cat, stage: sl.stage,
                  current: as ? as.title : ""
                }
              });
            },
            remove: function () {
              self.askConfirm({
                title: "Remove from featured?",
                body: "“" + as.title + "” will stop appearing in the " + sl.cat + " slot. The article itself is not deleted.",
                label: "Remove",
                run: function () {
                  const next = Object.assign({}, s.featAssign);
                  delete next[sl.key];
                  self.syncFeatured(next);
                  self.say("Removed from the " + sl.cat + " slot.");
                }
              });
            }
          };
        })
      };
    });

    // ---- docs
    const docQ = s.docQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    const docsList = DOCS.filter(function (d) {
      if (s.docDel.indexOf(d[0]) !== -1) return false;
      if (!libAllowed(docLibOf(d))) return false;
      if (s.docType !== "All types" && d[1].toUpperCase() !== s.docType) return false;
      if (s.docLib !== "All" && docLibOf(d) !== s.docLib) return false;
      if (docQ && d[0].toLowerCase().indexOf(docQ) === -1) return false;
      return true;
    });

    const pgArt = this.pgr(artRows, "pgArt", 8);
    const pgUsers = this.pgr(users, "pgUsers", 10);
    const pgDocs = this.pgr(docsList, "pgDocs", 10);
    const pgDb = this.pgr(dbSourceRows, "pgDb", 12);
    const pgTopics = this.pgr(topicRows, "pgTopics", 12);

    return {
      // shell
      role: s.role, roles: ROLES, roleBg: roleObj.bg, roleFg: roleObj.fg,
      navItems: navItems,
      sidebarBg: dark ? C.cta : "#FFFFFF", sidebarFg: dark ? C.ivory : C.char,
      sidebarRule: dark ? "rgba(255,255,255,.22)" : C.sand,
      sidebarW: rail ? "72px" : "238px",
      showBrand: !rail,
      navPad: rail ? "12px 12px 20px" : "14px 10px 20px",

        ";padding:" + (rail ? "0 12px" : "0 14px 0 20px"),
      railIcon: rail ? "M9 6l6 6-6 6 M4 5v14" : "M15 6l-6 6 6 6 M20 5v14",
      railTitle: rail ? "Expand menu" : "Collapse menu",
      railBtnStyle: (rail ? "margin:0 auto;" : "margin-left:auto;") + "width:32px;height:32px;flex:none;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:none;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;background:" +
        (dark ? "rgba(255,255,255,.18)" : C.linen) + ";color:" + (dark ? C.ivory : C.charL),
      toggleRail: function () { self.setState({ rail: !s.rail, accountOpen: false }); },
      navState: s.navOpen ? "open" : "closed",
      toggleNav: function () { self.setState({ navOpen: !s.navOpen, accountOpen: false, query: "" }); },
      closeNav: function () { self.setState({ navOpen: false }); },

        (rail ? "9px 0;justify-content:center" : "9px 12px") + ";color:" + (dark ? "rgba(255,255,255,.88)" : C.charL),
      listOpen: !!s.listDlg,
      listTitle: s.listDlg ? s.listDlg.title : "",
      listSubtitle: s.listDlg ? s.listDlg.subtitle : "",
      listItems: s.listDlg ? s.listDlg.items : [],
      listClose: function () { self.setState({ listDlg: null }); },
      stopClick: function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); },
      confirmOpen: !!s.confirm,
      confirmTitle: s.confirm ? s.confirm.title : "",
      confirmBody: s.confirm ? s.confirm.body : "",
      confirmLabel: s.confirm ? s.confirm.label : "Delete",
      confirmRun: function () {
        const c = s.confirm;
        self.setState({ confirm: null });
        if (c && c.run) c.run();
      },
      confirmCancel: function () { self.setState({ confirm: null }); },
      rowH: rowH, toast: s.toast,
      canPreview: self.canPreview(),
      onRole: function (e) {
        if (!self.canPreview()) { self.say("Only a Supervisor can preview other roles."); return; }
        const raw = e.target.value;
        const match = ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r.id === raw || r.label === raw; })[0];
        if (!match) return;
        const v = match.id;
        const okScreen = PERM[s.screen].indexOf(v) !== -1 ? s.screen : (PERM.dashboard.indexOf(v) !== -1 ? "dashboard" : "review");
        self.setState({ role: v, open: null, screen: okScreen, query: "", backfillOpen: false });
        self.say("Previewing the panel as " + match.label + ".");
      },
      meName: me.name, meInitials: me.initials, meEmail: me.email,
      meRoleText: (ROLES.filter(function (x) { return x.id === me.role; })[0] || ROLES[0]).label + " account",

      // ---- login slider
      slidePause: function () { self.set("slidePaused", true); },
      slideResume: function () { self.set("slidePaused", false); },
      loginSlides: LOGIN_SLIDES.map(function (sl, i) {
        const on = i === (s.slide || 0);
        return {
          kicker: sl.kicker, title: sl.title, body: sl.body,
          dotStyle: "width:26px;height:4px;border-radius:9999px;background:" + sl.color,
          tintStyle: "position:absolute;z-index:1;top:-22%;right:-16%;width:78%;height:96%;border-radius:9999px;pointer-events:none;" +

            ";transform:scale(" + (on ? "1" : ".82") + ")",
          style: "position:absolute;top:50%;left:0;right:0;transition:opacity .62s ease, transform .62s cubic-bezier(.22,.7,.2,1);" +
            "opacity:" + (on ? "1" : "0") + ";pointer-events:" + (on ? "auto" : "none") +
            ";transform:translateY(" + (on ? "-50%" : "calc(-50% + 22px)") + ")"
        };
      }),
      loginDots: LOGIN_SLIDES.map(function (sl, i) {
        const on = i === (s.slide || 0);
        return {
          label: sl.kicker,
          go: function () { self.setState({ slide: i }); },
          wrapStyle: "border:none;padding:0;cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden;border-radius:9999px;height:4px;background:" +
            (on ? "rgba(46,46,46,.15)" : sl.color) + ";opacity:" + (on ? "1" : ".38") +
            ";width:" + (on ? "44px" : "26px") + ";transition:width .45s ease, opacity .45s ease, background .45s ease",
          fillStyle: "display:block;width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:9999px;transform-origin:left center;background:" + sl.color +
            (on ? ";animation:bbFill " + SLIDE_MS + "ms linear forwards;animation-play-state:" + (s.slidePaused ? "paused" : "running")
                : ";transform:scaleX(1);animation:none")
        };
      }),

      // ---- login
      signedIn: !!s.signedIn,
      signedOut: !s.signedIn,
      loginEmail: s.loginEmail,
      loginPw: s.loginPw,
      loginErr: s.loginErr,
      loginBusy: s.loginBusy,
      onLoginEmail: function (e) { self.setState({ loginEmail: e.target.value, loginErr: "" }); },
      onLoginPw: function (e) { self.setState({ loginPw: e.target.value, loginErr: "" }); },
      loginEmailStyle: this.loginField(!!s.loginErr),
      loginBtnLabel: s.loginBusy ? "Signing in…" : "Sign in",
      loginBtnStyle: "margin-top:6px;width:100%;border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:12px 18px;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:" +
        (s.loginBusy ? "wait" : "pointer") + ";background:" + (s.loginBusy ? C.charL : C.cta) + ";color:" + C.ivory,
      demoAccounts: ["super_user", "content_editor", "medical_reviewer", "tech"].map(function (rid) {
        return self.staffAccounts().filter(function (a) { return a.role === rid; })[0];
      }).filter(Boolean).map(function (a) {
        const r = ROLES.filter(function (x) { return x.id === a.role; })[0];
        const on = s.loginEmail.trim().toLowerCase() === a.email.toLowerCase();
        return {
          label: r.label, email: a.email,
          style: "border-radius:9999px;padding:6px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid " +
            (on ? C.sage700 : C.sand) + ";background:" + (on ? C.sageSoft : "#fff") + ";color:" + (on ? C.sage900 : C.char),
          fill: function () { self.setState({ loginEmail: a.email, loginPw: s.staffPw[a.key] || "", loginErr: "" }); }
        };
      }),
      submitLogin: function (e) {
        if (e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
        if (s.loginBusy) return;
        const email = (s.loginEmail || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)) { self.set("loginErr", "Enter a valid email address."); return; }
        if (!s.loginPw) { self.set("loginErr", "Enter your password."); return; }
        const match = self.staffAccounts().filter(function (a) { return a.email.toLowerCase() === email; })[0];
        if (!match || s.staffPw[match.key] !== s.loginPw) { self.set("loginErr", "That email and password do not match an admin account. Ask a Supervisor to check your account in Team."); return; }
        if (s.suspended.indexOf(match.key) !== -1) { self.set("loginErr", "This account is suspended. Ask a Supervisor to reinstate it."); return; }
        self.setState({ loginBusy: true, loginErr: "" });
        setTimeout(function () {
          self.setState({
            loginBusy: false, signedIn: true, signedInEmail: match.email, signedInName: match.key, loginPw: "",
            role: match.role, screen: PERM.articles.indexOf(match.role) !== -1 ? "articles" : "review", accountOpen: false, open: null, query: ""
          });
          self.say("Signed in as " + match.name + " · " + self.roleLabel(match.role) + ".");
        }, 450);
      },
      backToSite: function () { window.location.href = "../index.html"; },
      accountOpen: s.accountOpen,
      accountStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:5px 9px 5px 5px;border-radius:9999px;cursor:pointer;background:" +
        (s.accountOpen ? C.ivory : "transparent") + ";border:1px solid " + (s.accountOpen ? C.sand : "transparent"),
      accountChevStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;transition:transform .18s ease;transform:rotate(" + (s.accountOpen ? "180deg" : "0deg") + ")",
      toggleAccount: function () { self.setState({ accountOpen: !s.accountOpen }); },
      openProfile: function () { self.setState({ accountOpen: false }); self.say("Opening your profile and account settings."); },
      onLogout: function () {
        self.setState({ signedIn: false, accountOpen: false, loginEmail: "", loginPw: "", loginErr: "", open: null, query: "", rail: false, teamUnlocked: false, teamPin: "", teamPinErr: "", pwShow: {}, pwEdit: null });
        self.say("You have been signed out.");
      },
      query: s.query,
      onQuery: function (e) { self.set("query", e.target.value); },
      searchOpen: q.length > 1, results: results, noResults: q.length > 1 && results.length === 0,

      noAccess: !allowed,
      noAccessText: "The " + roleObj.label + " role cannot reach this section." + (self.canPreview() ? " Change the preview role in the top bar to see how the panel differs." : ""),
      goDash: function () { self.go("dashboard"); },
      goMorph: function () { self.go("morpheus"); },
      goNew: function () { self.go("new"); },
      goPre: function () { self.go("pre"); },
      goArticles: function () { self.go("articles"); },

      isDash: allowed && s.screen === "dashboard",
      isArticles: allowed && s.screen === "articles",
      isNew: allowed && s.screen === "new",
      isMorph: allowed && s.screen === "morpheus",
      isPre: allowed && s.screen === "pre",
      isReview: allowed && s.screen === "review",
      isStories: allowed && s.screen === "stories",
      isUsers: allowed && s.screen === "users",
      isTeam: allowed && s.screen === "team",
      isDb: allowed && s.screen === "db",
      isDocs: allowed && s.screen === "docs",
      canNew: this.can("new"), canMorpheus: this.can("morpheus"), canPre: this.can("pre"),
      dashGrid: this.can("morpheus") ? "1.35fr 1fr" : "1fr",

      // dashboard
      pillarMix: (function () {
        const order = [
          { label: "Before pregnancy", match: "Before Pregnancy", color: C.before },
          { label: "Pregnancy", match: "Pregnancy", color: C.preg },
          { label: "Birth", match: "Birth", color: C.birth },
          { label: "First years", match: "First Years", color: C.first }
        ];
        const total = ARTICLES.length;
        const drill = self.can("articles");
        return order.map(function (o) {
          const n = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.stage === o.match; }).length;
          const pct = total ? Math.round((n / total) * 100) : 0;
          return {
            label: o.label, value: n + " · " + pct + "%",
            clickable: drill, static: !drill,
            title: drill ? "Show " + n + " " + o.label.toLowerCase() + " articles" : "",
            rowStyle: "display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:none;padding:6px 8px;margin:0 -8px 5px;border-radius:8px" + (drill ? ";cursor:pointer" : ""),
            barStyle: "height:100%;border-radius:5px;width:" + pct + "%;background:" + o.color,
            go: function () { self.setState({ screen: "articles", query: "", artQ: "", artStatus: "all", artStage: o.match, artTier: "all", artAspect: "all", open: null, accountOpen: false, pgArt: 1 }); }
          };
        });
      })(),
      tierMix: (function () {
        const drill = self.can("articles");
        return TIERS.map(function (t) {
          const n = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.tier === t.id; }).length;
          return {
            label: t.label === "Clinical Adjacent" ? "Clinical adj." : t.label, n: n, color: t.color,
            clickable: drill, static: !drill,
            title: drill ? "Show " + n + " " + t.label.toLowerCase() + " articles" : "",
            rowStyle: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;width:100%;background:none;border:none;padding:4px 8px;margin:0 -8px;border-radius:8px" + (drill ? ";cursor:pointer" : ""),
            go: function () { self.setState({ screen: "articles", query: "", artQ: "", artStatus: "all", artStage: "all", artTier: t.id, artAspect: "all", open: null, accountOpen: false, pgArt: 1 }); }
          };
        }).filter(function (t) { return t.n > 0; });
      })(),
      donutStyle: (function () {
        const total = ARTICLES.length;
        let acc = 0;
        const stops = TIERS.map(function (t) {
          const n = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.tier === t.id; }).length;
          if (!n) return "";
          const start = acc;
          acc += (n / total) * 360;
          return t.color + " " + start + "deg " + acc + "deg";
        }).filter(Boolean).join(", ");
        return "width:84px;height:84px;border-radius:9999px;flex:none;background:conic-gradient(" + stops + ");mask:radial-gradient(farthest-side,transparent calc(100% - 15px),#000 calc(100% - 15px));-webkit-mask:radial-gradient(farthest-side,transparent calc(100% - 15px),#000 calc(100% - 15px))";
      })(),
      dashSub: ARTICLES.length + " articles · " + activeCount + " active site users · previewing as " + roleObj.label,

      noAttentionNote: "Every queue your role can reach is empty.",
      pipeline: pipeline,
      activity: (function (list) {
        return list.map(function (ev, i) {
          return Object.assign({}, ev, {
            style: "display:flex;gap:12px;padding:13px 20px" + (i === list.length - 1 ? "" : ";border-bottom:1px solid " + C.linen)
          });
        });
      })([
        { text: "Loucas George approved “Your Hospital Bag for a Smooth Arrival”", meta: "Supervisor · 2 hours ago", color: C.sage700 },
        { text: "Morpheus generated a draft on third-trimester sleep", meta: "Content Studio · 4 hours ago", color: C.moira },
        { text: "“Exploring Adoption” was held off the live site", meta: "Supervisor · yesterday", color: C.clayM },

      ]),

      // articles — bulk aspect backfill. It edits live articles too, so it is limited to the roles that can publish.
      canBackfill: canPublish,

      backfillBtnLabel: s.backfillOpen ? "Close aspect backfill" : "Backfill aspect",

        (s.backfillOpen ? C.cta : "#fff") + ";color:" + (s.backfillOpen ? C.ivory : C.char) + (s.backfillOpen ? "" : ";border:1px solid " + C.sand),
      toggleBackfill: function () {
        if (!canPublish) { self.say("Only a Supervisor or Tech can backfill aspects."); return; }
        self.setState({ backfillOpen: !s.backfillOpen, aspectDraft: {} });
      },
      backfillOnlyMissing: s.backfillOnlyMissing,
      toggleOnlyMissing: function () { self.setState({ backfillOnlyMissing: !s.backfillOnlyMissing }); },
      backfillMissing: ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed"; }).length,

      backfillRows: (function () {
        const list = s.backfillOnlyMissing ? ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed"; }) : ARTICLES;
        return list.map(function (a) {
          const drafted = s.aspectDraft[a.title];
          const current = ASPECTS.find(function (o) { return o.label === a.aspect; });
          const value = drafted !== undefined ? drafted : (a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed" ? "" : (current ? current.value : ""));
          return {
            title: a.title, stage: a.stage, value: value,
            selectStyle: "height:32px;padding:0 8px;border:1px solid " + (value ? C.sand : C.clay) + ";border-radius:8px;background:#fff;font-size:12.5px;color:#2E2E2E;cursor:pointer;width:100%",
            onChange: function (e) { const d = Object.assign({}, s.aspectDraft); d[a.title] = e.target.value; self.setState({ aspectDraft: d }); }
          };
        });
      })(),
      backfillEmpty: s.backfillOnlyMissing && ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed"; }).length === 0,
      backfillHasRows: !(s.backfillOnlyMissing && ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed"; }).length === 0),
      saveAllDisabled: Object.keys(s.aspectDraft).filter(function (t) { return s.aspectDraft[t]; }).length === 0,
      saveAllStyle: "border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:8px 18px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:" +
        (Object.keys(s.aspectDraft).filter(function (t) { return s.aspectDraft[t]; }).length === 0 ? "not-allowed" : "pointer") + ";background:" +
        (Object.keys(s.aspectDraft).filter(function (t) { return s.aspectDraft[t]; }).length === 0 ? C.sand : C.cta) + ";color:" +
        (Object.keys(s.aspectDraft).filter(function (t) { return s.aspectDraft[t]; }).length === 0 ? C.mute : C.ivory),
      saveAllAspects: function () {

        const draft = s.aspectDraft;
        let n = 0;
        Object.keys(draft).forEach(function (title) {
          const val = draft[title];
          if (!val) return;
          const opt = ASPECTS.find(function (o) { return o.value === val; });
          const row = ARTICLES.find(function (a) { return a.title === title; });
          if (row && opt) { row.aspect = opt.label; if (row.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed") row.warn = ""; n++; }
        });
        const stillMissing = ARTICLES.filter(function (a) { return a.warn === "Aspect unconfirmed"; }).length;
        self.setState({ aspectDraft: {}, backfillOpen: s.backfillOnlyMissing && stillMissing === 0 ? false : s.backfillOpen });
        self.say("Updated the aspect on " + n + " article" + (n === 1 ? "" : "s") + ".");
      },

      // articles
      articlesSub: ARTICLES.length + " articles across four stages. Filter down, then open a row to act on it.",
      artStats: artStats, artFilters: artFilters, artRows: pgArt.items, artPager: pgArt.bar,
      artQ: s.artQ, onArtQ: function (e) { self.setState({ artQ: e.target.value, open: null, pgArt: 1 }); },
      artShowing: filtered.length + " of " + ARTICLES.length + " articles match",
      artFiltered: artFiltered, artEmpty: filtered.length === 0,
      clearArt: function () { self.setState({ artStatus: "all", artStage: "all", artTier: "all", artAspect: "all", artQ: "", open: null }); },

      // new article
      newTitle: s.draftTitle ? "Editing draft" : "New article",
      newSub: "Write on the left, classify on the right. Everything marked with an asterisk is required before it can leave your hands.",
      draftTitle: s.draftTitle,

            const nm = s.libNameDraft.trim();
            if (!nm) { self.say("A library needs a name."); return; }
            const nextLibs = s.libs.map(function (x) {
              return x.slug === l.slug ? Object.assign({}, x, { name: nm, desc: s.libDescDraft.trim() }) : x;
            });
            const nextMap = Object.assign({}, s.docLibMap);

              DOCS.forEach(function (d) { if (docLibOf(d) === l.name) nextMap[d[0]] = nm; });
            }

              libs: nextLibs, docLibMap: nextMap, libEdit: null,
              docLib: s.docLib === l.name ? nm : s.docLib,
              dbDocLib: s.dbDocLib === l.name ? nm : s.dbDocLib
            });
            self.say(nm + " saved.");
          },

            if (n > 0) { self.say("Move its " + n + (n === 1 ? " file" : " files") + " out before deleting this library."); return; }
            self.askConfirm({
              title: "Delete this library?",
              body: "“" + l.name + "” is empty and will be removed. This cannot be undone.",
              label: "Delete library",
              run: function () {
                const nextLibs = s.libs.filter(function (x) { return x.slug !== l.slug; });
                self.setState({
                  libs: nextLibs, libEdit: null,
                  docLib: s.docLib === l.name ? "All" : s.docLib,
                  dbDocLib: s.dbDocLib === l.name ? nextLibs[0].name : s.dbDocLib

          editType: function () {
            self.setState({
              pickerDlg: {
                title: "Document type",
                subtitle: full,
                options: DOC_TYPES.map(function (t) {
                  return {
                    label: t, note: "", on: t === type,
                    run: function () {
                      const next = Object.assign({}, s.docTypeMap);
                      next[d[0]] = t;
                      self.setState({ docTypeMap: next, pickerDlg: null });
                      self.say(full + " marked as " + t + ".");
                    }
                  };
                })
              }
            });
          },
          move: function () {
            self.setState({
              pickerDlg: {
                title: "Move to library",
                subtitle: full + " · currently in " + lib,
                options: libNames.map(function (l) {
                  return {
                    label: l, note: l === lib ? "Current library" : "", on: l === lib,
                    run: function () {
                      const next = Object.assign({}, s.docLibMap);
                      next[d[0]] = l;
                      self.setState({ docLibMap: next, pickerDlg: null });
                      self.say(full + " moved to " + l + ".");
                    }
                  };
                })
              }
            });
          },
          view: function () { self.say("Opening " + full + " in the viewer."); },
          download: function () { self.say(full + " downloaded."); },
          del: function () {
            self.askConfirm({
              title: "Delete this file?",
              body: "“" + full + "” will be removed from the " + lib + " library. This cannot be undone.",
              label: "Delete file",
              run: function () {
                self.setState({ docDel: s.docDel.concat([d[0]]) });
                self.say(full + " deleted.");
              }
            });
          }
        };
      }),
      noDocs: docsList.length === 0,
      pickerOpen: !!s.pickerDlg,
      pickerTitle: s.pickerDlg ? s.pickerDlg.title : "",
      pickerSubtitle: s.pickerDlg ? s.pickerDlg.subtitle : "",
      pickerOptions: s.pickerDlg ? s.pickerDlg.options.map(function (o) {
        return Object.assign({}, o, {
          hasNote: !!o.note,
          style: "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:12px 22px;border:none;border-bottom:1px solid " + C.linen +
            ";background:" + (o.on ? C.sage50 : "#fff") + ";cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;color:" + C.charD,
          markStyle: "margin-left:auto;flex:none;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:" + (o.on ? C.sage700 : "transparent")
        });
      }) : [],
      pickerClose: function () { self.setState({ pickerDlg: null }); },
      uploadDoc: function () { self.setState({ upOpen: true, upFile: null, upErr: "", upLib: s.docLib === "All" ? libNames[0] : s.docLib }); },
      upOpen: s.upOpen,
      upClose: function () { self.setState({ upOpen: false, upFile: null, upErr: "" }); },
      upHasFile: !!s.upFile,
      upNoFile: !s.upFile,
      upFileName: s.upFile ? s.upFile.name : "",
      upFileMeta: s.upFile ? s.upFile.ext.toUpperCase() + " · " + s.upFile.size : "",
      upErr: s.upErr, upHasErr: !!s.upErr,
      onUpPick: function (e) {
        const f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
        if (!f) return;
        const parts = f.name.split(".");
        const ext = parts.length > 1 ? parts.pop().toLowerCase() : "";
        const base = parts.join(".");
        const allowed = ["docx", "pdf", "md", "csv", "txt"];
        if (allowed.indexOf(ext) === -1) {
          self.setState({ upFile: null, upErr: "That file type is not accepted. Use DOCX, PDF, MD, CSV or TXT." });
          return;
        }
        if (f.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
          self.setState({ upFile: null, upErr: "That file is over the 10 MB limit." });
          return;
        }
        if (DOCS.some(function (d) { return d[0].toLowerCase() === base.toLowerCase(); })) {
          self.setState({ upFile: null, upErr: "A file with that name is already in the library." });
          return;
        }
        const kb = Math.max(1, Math.round(f.size / 1024));
        self.setState({
          upFile: { name: f.name, base: base, ext: ext, size: kb > 1024 ? (kb / 1024).toFixed(1) + " MB" : kb + " KB" },
          upErr: ""
        });
      },
      upLib: s.upLib,
      upLibs: libNames,
      onUpLib: function (e) { self.set("upLib", e.target.value); },
      upType: s.upType,
      upTypes: DOC_TYPES,
      onUpType: function (e) { self.set("upType", e.target.value); },
      upBtnStyle: "border:none;border-radius:9999px;padding:9px 20px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:" + (s.upFile ? "pointer" : "not-allowed") +
        ";background:" + (s.upFile ? C.cta : C.sand) + ";color:" + (s.upFile ? C.ivory : C.mute),
      runUpload: function () {
        if (!s.upFile) { self.setState({ upErr: "Choose a file first." }); return; }
        const f = s.upFile;
        DOCS.push([f.base, f.ext, s.upLib, f.size, "18 Aug"]);
        const nextTypes = Object.assign({}, s.docTypeMap);
        nextTypes[f.base] = s.upType;
        self.setState({ upOpen: false, upFile: null, upErr: "", docTypeMap: nextTypes, docLib: s.upLib, pgDocs: 1 });
        self.say(f.name + " uploaded to " + s.upLib + ".");
      },
      ...this.v5vals(),
      ...this.v5editor(),
      ...this.v5more()
    };
  }
}
</script>


</body></html>
